- **1915.03** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Anna Moser的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Klara坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Anna Moser把桶一放，夹起Klara进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josef Bauer赶来时，Klara正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food_

- **1916.12** `statistical_infection`

- **1917.03** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1917.06** `statistical_recovery`

- **1931.01** `major-family.childbirth/childbirth.daughter-in-a-drawer` tags: family, childbirth, household, major, lifecycle, birth, injury, partnership
  Klara在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个女儿，接生婆把她放在一只铺了旧衬衫的抽屉里。Hermann Weiss在走廊烧水，把家里唯一干净的毛巾递进来；接生婆收下六克朗，顺手把水盆端出去。孩子哭到天亮，Klara数她的手指，数了两遍，才相信都是齐的。

  成绩过了，厂里却没有正式空缺。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne让Klara先做三个月替工，按日结算，一个月四十八克朗，出师纸压在账房抽屉里。Berta Hofer说等秋天有人走，再谈正式位置；Klara把工具袋搬到自己那张案子下面。
  _effects: education apprenticeship; employment Aushilfssetzer @ org-winter-print; role; flag +temporary_journeyman; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39_

  Klara在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Klara的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  Klara嫌荐书写得太轻，自己在末尾添了一句“能独立看图纸”。新厂的Maria Moser 3让Klara当场画一张简单的装配图，Klara画到一半就停了。Maria Moser 3把信举起来，问最后那句是谁写的；Klara接过信，没有回答。
  _effects: cash -2 (旧工头的一封荐书（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.07999999999999996; reputation -0.07999999999999999; flag +work13_forged_reference_caught; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-60; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-93_

- **1936.06** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.rent-note/rent-note.together-to-office` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara和Emil Weiss叫上楼下两家，一同去房东的事务所。Franz Lechner 2隔着柜台听他们说完，翻出账簿，答应先补上楼梯间的窗闩，涨租从下下个月起算。四人出门时挤在台阶上，谁也没先说话，Emil Weiss把帽子往后一推，说总算没白跑。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; mood 0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1937.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sinecure-created` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Klara在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Mitzi Huber第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Gustav Berg发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:005698; organization_funds; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17; flag +private_sinecure; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-17_

- **1937.01** `kernel-health.task-tuberculosis-cure::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::tb-cure-war-requisition/tb-war-home-cure` tags: health, task, tuberculosis, sanatorium, cure, vienna, urban, crisis
  疗养院被改成军医院，病床让给了伤员。Klara领到一瓶鱼肝油和一张“居家隔离”的纸条；医生说，窗户开着，别对着人咳。他把窗户开了一条缝，又把床挪到离门最远的地方。鱼肝油见底后，他把瓶子倒过来控了控。
  _effects: cash -3 (cod-liver oil and a written home-isolation note（其中3克朗记入债务）); condition; role; knowledge “战争一来，疗养院先改成军医院；肺病病人领一瓶鱼肝油回家，窗户开着，别对着人咳。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1937.09** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.sick-parent-night/hospital-deposit` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara跑了一趟医院，把Josef Bauer送进病房：押金三克朗，搪瓷杯和旧毛巾一克朗二十赫勒，头三天的药费三克朗八十赫勒，一共八克朗。护士收走便盆，换上带刻度的玻璃壶。Klara回到空了的床边，把塌下去的椅子扶正，才发现自己一整天只喝了一杯咖啡。
  _effects: cash -8 (住院押金和住院用品（其中8克朗记入债务）); health 0.030000000000000027 (父亲住进医院得到治疗); mood -0.05999999999999994; flag +parent_hospitalized_

- **1937.10** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Klara看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  Klara在登记桌旁说了句“这里以前不是这样”。Friedrich Hofer记下地址，当晚两个穿制服的人来敲门，把Klara带到警局，第二天放回来时脸上有伤。
  _effects: condition; role; cash -5 (fine and lost work)_

- **1938.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/difficult-birth` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, state, state:raising-child, raising-child
  Klara难产，接生的人守了两天。孩子最后生下来，取名Rosa Moser；Klara一直发烧，医院又收走二十克朗，Ferdinand Moser把钟表送进当铺。Klara落下产后虚弱的毛病，抱孩子时手臂发抖，站久了眼前就发黑。
  _effects: birth female; cash -20 (midwife and hospital); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:239_

  Klara喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Mitzi Winter在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000326:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1939.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Klara的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  Klara在靴筒夹层里摸到一张折成小块的纸，是部队的调动命令。名誉委员会看见了，说烧掉，别让太太知道。Klara把纸投进炉子，火苗舔到指尖。第二天，名誉委员会把一双旧靴子赏给Klara，尺码大了两号。
  _effects: cash 3 (靴筒里的一枚砂砾); mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +burned_mobilization_order; knowledge “the master hid a transfer order in his boot”_

  _effects: reputation -0.06; mood -0.07999999999999996; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27_

  _effects: flag +wrote_denunciation_letter; knowledge “the denunciation letter was forwarded to the block warden”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

  在第二个桥洞下，Berta Winter 2先停下来，用鞋尖碾一颗烤栗子壳，说下星期天还是这个时候、还是这座桥。Klara点头，掏出四赫勒买了两小袋栗子，两人分着剥，手上都是糖渍；桥洞上的水还在滴，谁也没有提万一明天下雨怎么办。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; cash -0.04 (两小袋烤栗子); mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105_

- **1949.01** `p9-history-public-action.speak-meeting/shouted-down` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  嘘声从靠墙那排先起来，很快盖过 Klara 的声音。有人把帽子摔在长凳上，Grete Berg 站起来拍手让大家安静，桌角的啤酒瓶被碰倒，淌了一小片。
  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009; reputation -0.040000000000000036; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

- **1949.03** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.breakup/stay-for-the-rent` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  东西还到一半，两人算了算房租、煤钱和两个人的工钱凑在一起才够。最后钥匙没收回去，外套也留下；这个月能少花一克朗，话说到“以后”两个字就断了，谁都不往下接，炉子今晚还是得两个人一起生。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-105; cash 1 (合租省下一份房租); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1949.09** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/drops-and-endure` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药房伙计从褐色瓶里倒出药水，叮嘱别咽。Mitzi Winter在柜台边说，这玩意儿治标不治本。Klara把棉球塞进嘴里，腮帮子鼓出一块，走回巷子时拿手帕捂着脸，怕风吹。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (牙痛药水); condition_

- **1949.09** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.stairwell-rumor/stairwell-rumor.warn-the-latch` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Klara没传话，只在二楼门口站了一会儿，提醒Friedrich Winter 2夜里把门闩好，说楼梯间有人爱嚼舌头。Friedrich Winter 2点点头，把门闩推过去，发出很轻的一声。此后送活的人还是照来，闲话却渐渐没人接。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-30; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-121_

- **1949.11** `p9-social-family-conflict-care.christmas-soup/jam-at-door` tags: family, care, siblings, inheritance, housing, money
  Klara只送来一罐杏子果酱，没有进门，把罐子放在门槛里边，说了句汤你们先喝。Josef在屋里听见了，走到门口时人已经下楼。果酱罐上没有标签，只裹着一层报纸，瓶口用蜡封着；这一罐甜食抵得上一克朗的吃食，却没能把两个人重新按到一张桌上。
  _effects: food; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.join-second-row` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Klara把两克朗月费和徽章钱一起放在啤酒渍斑斑的桌上，Anna Berg在名册上添了一行。钢琴缺的那个音在排练中途又卡住，Klara站在第二排跟着哼，声音被两边的人盖住，胸腔里却震得发麻；散场时能把副歌完整唱下来，也记住了怎么在人群里找准自己的声部。
  _effects: cash -2 (合唱团月费和铜徽章钱); mood 0.07000000000000006; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; skill_

- **1950.05** `vienna-life-overcrowded-flat/apply-for-a-municipal-flat` tags: housing, home, health, family
  Klara去市政办事处登记申请更大的市营住宅。办事员收下表格和两克朗手续费，说前面还有几百户，至少要等两年；Klara把回执折好，回家路上看见新盖的市营楼房，窗户亮着，门却还进不去。
  _effects: cash -2 (municipal housing application); role_

- **1950.06** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_no_damage` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Klara刚把锅端下灶，火苗就蹿了起来，Franz Winter 2一脚踩灭地上的油布，顺手抄起沙箱里的沙子盖住灶口。两人都没受伤，但Franz Winter 2的鞋底烧穿一个洞，那是唯一一双出门的鞋。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; mood -0.050000000000000044_

- **1950.10** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.refuse` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Klara说这个月还能撑，房租差的那几克朗打算替人洗衣补上，请Alois Berg把篮子提回去给更困难的人家。Alois Berg在门口停了一会儿，把一小块肥皂留在窗台上，说不是救济，是教区多出来的。门关上后，屋里只剩煤箱底刮出的响声。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84; flag +parish_refused_aid; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1950.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Klara就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Franz Winter 2摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.05 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

  Klara看Franz Winter 2盆里那几件小衬衫，把自家床单卷起来抱回屋，说等下午再晾。Franz Winter 2愣了一下，闷头把衣服挂好，收工回来时在Klara门口放了一小包用旧报纸裹的洗衣碱。Klara打开报纸，碱面里还掺着几粒粗盐。
  _effects: relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; food_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013426_

- **1951.01** `r15-wish.found-faction::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/founded` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, office, status, vienna, urban
  Klara和Anna Hofer在咖啡馆后间成立了一个小小的政治俱乐部。桌子是借的，章程是手写的，但签名、印章和一间可以锁门的办公室是真的。Maria Weiss没有到场，只送来一句“先看看你们能撑多久”。
  _effects: cash -2 (一间借来的办公室（其中1.55克朗记入债务）); reputation 0.05999999999999994; organization_found 权力俱乐部 (party); position 发起人 @org:r15-faction:evt:world-vienna-1912:013947_

  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:013947_

  _effects: cash -0.4 (诊所挂号费); knowledge “综合医院的药房比街角药房便宜，但要排到下午”_

  _effects: cash 6 (板车上的十二箱); flag +work13_delivered_goods_on_time; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-104_

  计票拖到凌晨，Klara以两票之差留任。Hermann Huber把已经写好的交接清单折起来，笑着说下次再来。Klara回到办公室，发现桌上的文件被人重新排过；最上面那份，是新一年的预算草案。
  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005; mood 0.06000000000000005; organization_funds; state_add office-patronage state:world-vienna-1912:516; flag +reappointed; knowledge “桌上的文件被人重新排过，最上面是新一年的预算草案。”; flag +office_patronage_network_

- **1954.07** `vienna-women-housework.coal-stove/coal-stove.borrow-fire` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family
  Klara端着一只旧锅去敲Gustav Moser的门，想借几块烧红的炭。Gustav Moser让她从自家炉膛里夹了一块，又抓了一小把引火的木片。Klara连声道谢，回来把炭放进炉栅，火是有了，屋里却还是冷得能呵出白气。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; food; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1954.07** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/weathered` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  报纸在头版登了Klara和承包商在一张桌子上的照片，配文只有一句“谁签的字”。质询会开了三个小时，Klara把合同、比价单和验收记录一页页摊开；记者第二天没有再写，但把照片留在了档案里。
  _effects: reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.030000000000000027; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:515; flag +scandal_weathered; knowledge “照片是真的，合同也是真的；报纸没有再追，但档案里留了底片。”; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1955.01** `r15-wish.power-offer::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/real-appointment` tags: politics, power, wish, office, status, vienna, urban, postwar
  Hermann Huber把一份任命书推到Klara面前。职位不大，但有印章、有预算、有能签字的地方。Klara签下名字时，第一次觉得那件一直想着的事有了一个可以摸到的边。
  _effects: reputation 0.05999999999999961; position 部门主管 @org-sdap_

- **1955.01** `state_tick:office-under-siege/pressure-year/held-on` tags: office-under-siege, office, scandal, pressure, politics, legal, risk, state
  质询会开了两个小时，Klara把每一笔签字都念了一遍。没有人被说服，但也没有人再追问；散会时，支持Klara的人比进门时多了两个。
  _effects: state_parameter office-under-siege.support 60 -> 65; state_parameter office-under-siege.exposure 30 -> 26; state_parameter office-under-siege.months 0 -> 1; mood 0.010000000000000009_

  _effects: state_parameter office-patronage.exposure 15 -> 27; state_parameter office-patronage.loyalty 55 -> 49; reputation -0.030000000000000027; mood -0.010000000000000009; flag +patronage_rumor_

- **1955.05** `r1-cul.funeral-sermon::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/unvarnished-eulogy` tags: faith, funeral, family, secrets, grief, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara替Otto Winter 2写悼词，写到第三段时发现没有一句真话。Klara把稿子揉了，走上台讲死者怎么在冬天把最后一块煤塞进Klara的鞋里。台下有人哭，Josef没有哭，只把一朵白花放在棺木上。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; flag +spoke_unvarnished_eulogy; role; relation colleague with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; knowledge “the dead man put his last coal in the child’s boot”_

- **1955.08** `vienna-market-debt.pawn-watch/pawn-whole-watch` tags: market, debt, money, home, shop
  你把银壳怀表推到绒布托盘上，当铺掌柜用放大镜扫了一眼机芯，报出八克朗。你盯着那张窄票上模糊的赎当日期，想起父亲在第十区工棚里上弦的样子。邻居在门外咳嗽，你捏着票和钱走出来，先拐去房东那里付了半个月的欠租。
  _effects: cash 8 (典当银壳怀表所得); mood -0.02000000000000013; flag +watch_pawned_

- **1955.11** `r3-mig.xenophobic-landlord::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/landlord-called-police` tags: migration, housing, hostility, landlord, poverty, vienna, urban, postwar
  Klara拒绝加租，Luise Kral第二天带着警察来，说屋里有六个人登记，实际住了九个。Berta Bauer 4站在楼梯口，说愿意替Klara作证只住了四个。警察数了数床垫，把数字写在本子上，房东在旁边补充“他们晚上还烧煤油炉”。
  _effects: mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +landlord_reported_overcrowding; relation rival with person-luise-kral; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; knowledge “the landlord counts mattresses for the police”_

  一个快要散掉的政治俱乐部让Klara接手。会员只剩十一个人，欠着两个月房租，但那间办公室的门牌还挂着。Klara在第一次会上没有讲理想，只把账本翻开。
  _effects: reputation 0.015000000000000013; organization_found 接手后的俱乐部 (party); position 临时主席 @org:r15-club:evt:world-vienna-1912:017114_

- **1956.04** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Klara看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-84_

- **1956.12** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Otto Hofer排在前面，回头替Klara看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Klara用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:002142:child; mood 0.030000000000000027_

  Maria Weiss在会议上先一步拿到了多数。Klara的提案被改成一段附注，连名字都没有留下。散会后，Klara在走廊里站了一会儿，然后去下一间办公室。
  _effects: reputation -0.050000000000000044; state_add political-suspect state:world-vienna-1912:567_

  _effects: reputation 0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:017538_

- **1957.01** `r15-wish.faction-price::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/grew` tags: politics, power, wish, organization, intrigue, office, vienna, urban
  俱乐部从十一人变成四十人。Klara仍然坐主席的位置，但每次开会前都要先和Grete 2对一遍名单。有人开始叫Klara“主席”，也有人只在需要签字时才来。
  _effects: reputation 0.030000000000000027; knowledge “俱乐部扩大后，真正的权力不在主席的椅子上，而在会前对名单的修改。”_

- **1957.01** `curse15-bargain.borrowed-name::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/a-key-and-a-title` tags: wish, power, curse, ritual, bargain, office, irony, vienna
  办事处后门的Maria Weiss把一份旧名册翻到空页，让Klara在“会长”后面签下名字。三天后，Klara真的收到一枚铜钥匙和一张聘书：夜校同学会会长，职责是保管储物间。同学会只有三个成员，储物间的锁已经坏了。Maria Weiss没有再出现。

  Berta Stein 2把剩下的香肠切开，闻了闻，说“是肉坏了，不是人坏了”。Lena不肯信，Berta Stein 2把显微镜下的片子推过去，说“你要抓就抓那个卖香肠的”。Berta Bauer 4在病床上笑了一声，Klara却坐在椅子上哭了。
  _effects: mood 0.07999999999999996; flag +poisoning_charge_dropped; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94; knowledge “the sausage was contaminated with ptomaine”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-94_

  _effects: role_

- **1957.07** `vienna-historical-social-movement.district-list/demand-open-books` tags: history, historical_process, social_movement, organization, politics

  Klara点着煤油灯缝到后半夜，把一捆领子全做完了。针脚密是密，可天亮时她看东西重影，手指被针扎了三回。星期六交活，Josef Huber量了量尺寸，收下整捆，数出七十五赫勒推过桌面。

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; mood 0.040000000000000036_

  Klara和Josef Bauer 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Klara家，钥匙只在白天给。Josef Bauer 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Friedrich Weiss 2听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Klara把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。

  Friedrich Hofer说跨区转学要交“校舍维护费”，五克朗。Mitzi家拿不出，Friedrich Hofer把材料推到一边，叫下一个。Mitzi在走廊里站到下班，最后把父亲的工作证复印件塞进窗口，说父亲在印刷厂交税。Friedrich Hofer收了，章却盖在“待核”栏。开学一个月后，“待核”才改成“准”，Mitzi插班时课程已经讲到第三章。
  _effects: cash -4 (跨区转学的居住证明（其中4克朗记入债务）); mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +school_transfer_refused_

- **1917.10** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-win` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  巷子窄，两个孩子并排推着铁环，铁环撞在一起叮当作响。Josef的钩子先绕过最后一块界石。Mitzi Lechner不服，说再比一次，手却已经伸进口袋，摸出三颗彩色弹珠。Josef把弹珠举到眼前看，Mitzi Lechner在旁边踢墙，踢得鞋尖发白。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood 0.05; skill_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Berta Weiss 2在绳子那头喊。Josef踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Berta Weiss 2数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Josef把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-both-families` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  队伍拐弯时，Grete Hofer朝Josef招手。Josef挤过去，替两家把面包领出来，纸包用不同的绳结系住。家里人晚上解开绳结，先掂了掂份量，再问价钱；Josef把每一包的面包数、找零和欠的铜板都说了一遍，家里人听完才把两包分别收进柜子。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

  _effects: mood -0.08; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26; condition; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-26_

  _effects: mood 0.08000000000000002; flag +joined_roof_group; role; relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1930.01** `major-work-first-employment/hired-regular` tags: work, employment, unemployment, major
  清晨六点，印刷厂门口已经站了十几个人。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的工头让Josef把手摊开，看了看指节，又问能不能读一页纸。Josef读完，工头在名册上写了名字：明天六点，带工票和一双干净的手。第一周洗墨辊，月底工资三十克朗，柜上先预支四克朗。
  _effects: employment Druckerhelfer @ org-winter-print; role; cash 4 (first wage advance); relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +first_regular_job; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  Josef把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Grete Stein 2没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Josef回头看了一眼，Grete Stein 2正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47_

  Josef说自己也等钱交房租，把口袋翻给Alois Hofer看。Alois Hofer没有再求，转身去敲另一扇门；第二天，楼道里有人说Josef见死不救，Josef听见了，只把煤筐往门里挪了挪。那天夜里，两家都早早关了灯。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; role; reputation -0.03; flag +loan_refused; knowledge “邻居转身去敲了别人的门，楼道里开始议论这件事。”_

- **1930.06** `vienna-market-household.friday-fish/friday-fish.buy` tags: home, market, food, social
  鱼摊快收了，Josef挑了一条最小的鳟鱼，摊主顺手抓了把欧芹丢进纸里。黄油在锅里化开，鱼皮卷起来，楼道里都是味。Josef Hofer 2敲门时，Josef从鱼尾切下一小块递过去。
  _effects: cash -6 (买鳟鱼和黄油); food; mood 0.08000000000000002; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-50_

- **1930.10** `vienna-courtship-courtyard.family-table/nod-and-pots` tags: adulthood, courtship, partner, social, family, partnership
  汤喝到一半，Josef Bauer放下勺子点了头，但说得很清楚：先攒出一套锅碗、一只挂钟，再把话往下说。Grete Stein 2在桌下用膝盖碰了碰Josef，Josef Bauer把最后一块煮牛肉推过来，饭桌上的空气总算松了；这一顿让对方家里管了饭，自家灶上省下两克朗的吃食，也吃出一个带条件的准信。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; relation kin with person-josef-bauer; food_

  Josef没有把成品卖出去，而是搬回了家。家里有人说这能换钱，Josef说先放着。那张桌子后来用了很多年。
  _effects: mood 0.05; flag +kept_first_masterwork_

- **1931.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/settle-the-account` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  七点刚过，Hermann Weiss照例来买一只 Semmel。Josef把紫罗兰和两只 Semmel 放在柜台上，先付了四十赫勒，又把上回算错的账一笔一笔说清：借出去的是八克朗，记成了十克朗，是自己记错了。Hermann Weiss听完没马上说话，把零钱数进围裙口袋，最后说中午过来喝碗汤。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一小束紫罗兰和两只 Semmel); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; mood 0.08; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-22_

  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (手掌被滚烫铅字烫伤); cash 1.2 (当日未扣工的计件工资); reputation -0.06_

  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-47; mood -0.05_

- **1931.09** `vienna-legacy-worklife.apprentice-wage/apprentice-wage.ask-master` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  掌柜的木尺敲了两下柜台，声音短促。Josef把问题又问了一遍，掌柜才说师兄是“老规矩进来的”。师兄这时转过身，笑了一下，笑得不太自然，说“没事，你问得对”。
  _effects: reputation -0.04; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

- **1932.01** `vienna-life-work-injury/crushed-hand` tags: adulthood, labour, injury
  Josef在换纸时左手被压板夹住。工友关掉机器，工头拿来急救箱，把伤手包起来送去医院；厂里先垫了十克朗医药费，事故报告写的是“操作不慎”。Josef的左手从此握不紧拳，指尖总是凉的，天阴时骨缝里发疼。
  _effects: health -0.24 (crushed left hand); condition; cash 10 (employer medical advance)_

  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

  _effects: reputation -0.049999999999999996; cash -4.5 (停班一周少拿的工钱); health -0.050000000000000044 (受伤的腿没得到及时处理); relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-39; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

  _effects: unemployment (laid off in the depression); cash -5 (lost wages); condition_

- **1933.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/leave-vienna` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership, migration
  两张车票分开买，Josef在月台这头，Anton Winter 2在那头。火车开动后两人才在车厢连接处站到一起，谁也没有说话，只把行李往同一张座位底下推。

  医生在午后进门，先看了看炉子，再听胸口。他说是肺炎，留下三包药粉和一张账单；Emil Weiss替Josef把医生送到门口，回来时把药按顿分好。邻居把账单压在钟底下，说这钱先别急着还，药要按时吃。
  _effects: cash -12 (house call, medicine powder and a certificate); health 0.040000000000000036 (pneumonia treated at home by a doctor); condition; role; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss; knowledge “医生出诊先看炉子再听胸口，三包药粉和一张账单就是全部治疗。”_

- **1933.03** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.shared_stove_fire/fire_scald_hand` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  Josef在合租房走廊的公用灶台上煮土豆，转身取盐时袖子扫翻了铁锅，滚水浇在左手背上。Otto Stein从隔壁冲出来，扯下自己的围裙裹住烫伤处，又跑下楼叫来住在二楼的接生婆。接生婆用蛋清和旧亚麻布包扎，嘱咐三天不能沾水。
  _effects: health -0.050000000000000044 (左手背烫伤); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1933.09** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Haus der Tante in Klosterneuburg病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1933.12** `vienna-medical-treatment.toothache/barber-pull` tags: health, medical, family, money
  理发铺里还坐着等刮脸的老主顾，肥皂味和烟味混在一起。弗朗茨把围布往Josef胸前一系，说忍着点。钳子第一次滑脱，第二次才夹稳，牙出来时带着一小块肉，他用明矾水按住了。
  _effects: cash -1.2 (理发师拔牙收费); health 0.030000000000000027 (病牙被拔除但手法粗糙); mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_

- **1933.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`

- **1934.01** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.laundry_line_dispute/neighbor_yanks_clothes` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Otto Stein嫌Josef的床单滴水滴到Otto Stein家衬衫上，一把扯下床单扔在院子的石板上。Josef下楼捡起来时，床单角沾了泥，重洗又要费肥皂。两人在院子当中对喊，楼上有人推开窗看热闹。Josef把床单重新搭上绳子时，故意把水拧在Otto Stein那侧。

  手推车每走十几步就要停一下，楼道里的门半开着，有人探头看热闹。Josef把照片铁盒交给Maria Huber抱着，自己低头把最后一箱锅具拖上楼。天擦黑时，新屋里才勉强归置出下脚的地方。

  _effects: relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-69; mood -0.08999999999999997; flag +courtship_ended_cleanly; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-29_

- **1939.01** `state_tick:mb17-remittance-web/remittance-year/month-behind` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  这个月厂里少发了工钱。Josef把汇款单填到一半又放下，先给房东送去一部分；夜里他给故乡写信，说“下个月一定补上”，写完把信纸折了三折，像怕它自己会开口。
  _effects: cash -1 (the rent was paid first); state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 11 -> 12; state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.obligation 73 -> 77; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1939.01** `r13-civ.clinic-first-visit::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/dismissed-as-nerves` tags: health, clinic, queue, diagnosis, institution, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef把夜里咳醒的次数写在一张包装纸上，Elisabeth Berg 2扫了一眼，说是心事太重，给了几包安神粉。走廊尽头的护士压低声音，说住院部还有空床，但要医生签字。Josef把包装纸折回去，没再问。
  _effects: cash -1 (诊所走廊的第三张长凳); mood -0.07999999999999996; flag +clinic_dismissed_as_nerves; knowledge “the clinic doctor refused a hospital referral”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90_

- **1939.03** `vienna-legacy-worklife.sick-fund/sick-fund.keep-quiet` tags: legacy, work, family, reform, ordinary_life
  止咳药水是褐色的，瓶口塞着软木塞，摇一摇会起沫。Josef看着工友把表折成小块，折了三折才塞进帽檐。墙上的规章还贴在那里，纸角被风掀起，没人去按。
  _effects: mood -0.050000000000000044; relation colleague with person-franz-lechner_

- **1939.03** `r13-turn.religious-conversion::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/baptised` tags: faith, religion, conversion, community, identity, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Weiss把Josef领到池边，问了三遍是否愿意。Josef第三遍才答“愿意”，声音比前两遍低。水从额头上浇下来时，最后一排有人站起来，又坐下。散礼后，Josef Weiss在名册上写下Josef的名字，说从今天起，这个堂区会替你记着。
  _effects: cash -2 (洗礼池边的迟疑); condition; flag +baptised_as_an_adult; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1939.08** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state
  边境又收紧了，往故乡的列车要换乘三次。邮局说汇款还能走，但信可能压在路上；Josef还是把两张单子一起递进窗口，像把一句话交给一个不保证送达的人。
  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 9 -> 11; mood -0.040000000000000036; flag +mb17_border_slow_letters_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:007756_

- **1939.10** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1939.12** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1940.01** `vienna-family-courtship/ask-to-share-a-home` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Anna Berg的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67_

- **1940.01** `wc17-commission.masterwork::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/commission-withdrawn` tags: craft, commission, masterwork, workshop, reputation, patronage, vienna, urban
  主顾在交货前改了主意：城里时兴更轻的样式，胡桃木太沉。Josef把做好的柜门卸下来，木料退不掉，只能抵给料行。Franz 2问还做不做，Josef说先把欠账还清。
  _effects: cash -2 (materials that could not be returned); mood -0.050000000000000044; flag +commission_lost; knowledge “胡桃木柜门已经做好，主顾却改要轻样式，木料退不回料行。”_

  Josef被叫到学校，老师说Katharina Weiss这学期总在课上看窗外。回家的路上，Josef没有骂，只把买好的铅笔又数了一遍；晚饭时，两个人都吃得很少。
  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 61 -> 56; state_parameter raising-child.caregiverBurden 33 -> 38; cash -2 (extra lessons and replacement pencils); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1940.01** `r13-fam.visit-grave::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/flowers-placed` tags: task, family, grave, mourning, neighbor, grief, vienna, urban
  Josef陪Helene Moser去公墓，把一把野菊放在新翻的土上。木牌上的名字被雨冲得发白，Helene Moser用手指一笔一画描了一遍，描到最后一笔，指腹上全是泥。Josef没有劝，只蹲下来把歪掉的木牌扶正。
  _effects: cash -1 (公墓新翻的土); condition; flag +visited_a_grave_with_neighbour; role; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-49_

  _effects: state_parameter raising-child.schoolProgress 31 -> 34; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Josef熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1940.09** `kernel-health.mental-breakdown::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::mental-crisis-police-transport/mental-crisis-lost-room` tags: health, mental, exhaustion, asylum, crisis, vienna, urban, war
  工厂的人来问过两次，房东随后把Josef的箱子搬到楼梯口。Josef在车站的长椅上坐到天亮，把药瓶倒空，又一颗颗捡回来；清洁工给了半杯温水。清洁工把拖把靠在长椅边，没有催他走。
  _effects: move place-wien; cash -4 (lost room deposit and food); condition; role; knowledge “工厂来问过两次以后，房东就把箱子搬到了楼梯口；车站长椅可以过夜。”_

- **1941.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction, partnership, household
  Anna Berg在几天后找到Josef，说可以试试。两人开始合付房租，也把各自欠的账摊在桌上。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-67; household; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:008360_

- **1941.01** `vienna-family-courtship/wait-for-a-better-year` tags: family, courtship, household, social, lifecycle
  Josef把话咽回去，只说等明年工钱稳一点再谈。Berta Bauer 3没有追问，两人把桌上的面包分完，各自去上工；出门时Josef回头看了一眼，Berta Bauer 3正把掉在桌上的面包屑拢进掌心，谁也没有把那个话头重新拾起来。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-97_

- **1941.01** `r13-civ.recover-at-home::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/neighbor-soup` tags: health, recovery, care, household, food, vienna, urban, crisis
  Ferdinand Winter端来一碗兑了水的牛奶，顺手把Josef家的炉子捅旺。Josef靠在床头，听Ferdinand Winter讲楼下谁也被传染了，谁家把病人送去了医院。人走后，屋里还留着一点煤烟和奶味。
  _effects: cash -1 (退烧后的第一碗粥（其中1克朗记入债务）); flag +recovered_with_neighbor_help; relation friend with person-ferdinand-winter_

- **1941.04** `state_reaction:mb17-remittance-web/border-closes` tags: mb17-remittance-web, migration, remittance, household, obligation, money, diaspora, state

  _effects: state_parameter mb17-remittance-web.arrears 12 -> 12; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.06** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.help-wash` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef喝完汤没走，留下来帮修女把大锅刷净，又把木碗按大小摞好。厨房后头有半块面包和一碗剩汤，修女说是帮忙人的午饭。Franz Huber在门口等着一起回去，说下回还来，洗锅的活总有人要干。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1941.11** `p9-social-tenement-neighbors.evicted-neighbours/evicted-neighbours.only-hot-water` tags: housing, neighbors, tenement, daily, money, reputation
  Josef没有开门，只从门缝递出一壶热水，说自家也挤不下。走廊里的箱子到半夜还堆着，孩子的哭声隔着门板一阵一阵。Josef坐在桌边没有点灯，第二天在楼梯间遇见时避开了目光。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-51_

- **1941.12** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.misdelivered-letter/misdelivered-letter.opened` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, letter
  信封没封牢，Josef在灯下把它掀开，里面是维也纳联合银行的催款单，欠了三十四克朗。她认出那名字是楼上常穿黑裙的女人，便把纸原样叠回去，用米汤把封口粘好，放到门房窗台上。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +knows_upstairs_debt_

- **1942.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Josef没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth（其中2克朗记入债务）); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:006116_

  姐姐把孩子裹在披肩里抱上电车，说城外有院子，一个月就送回来。Josef站在站台上看车走远。一个月后孩子会叫人了，可看见Josef就往姐姐身后躲。Josef把孩子抱进屋，他哭了半宿。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1942.03** `r3-mig.ship-passage::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/steerage-in-a-storm` tags: migration, ship, steerage, travel, health, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef的铺位在舱口下面，白天盖板一关，空气里全是煤灰和呕吐物的味道。第三天起风，船横着走了半天，Otto Winter 2把一桶海水泼在甲板上，说“吐完就好了”。Josef抓着床架，听见螺旋桨露出水面的声音。
  _effects: health -0.08000000000000002 (甲板下的煤灰); condition; flag +steerage_in_storm; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24; knowledge “the steerage hatch is closed in heavy weather”_

  邻居用麻绳把锅和铺盖捆在一起，打了个死结。他说城南有更便宜的屋子，但离厂远。你蹲在地上把最后几件衣服塞进布袋，听见看门人在楼下把垃圾桶挪来挪去。
  _effects: cash 2.8 (卖旧家具所得); mood -0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-emil-weiss_

- **1943.01** `interaction_response` tags: interaction

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:001878:child; interaction_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:009366_

- **1943.01** `hist15-occ.requisition-notice::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/name-the-neighbor` tags: occupation, requisition, shortage, institution, history, family, vienna, urban
  Josef告诉Franz Lechner，Berta Berg家的地窖里还有两桶猪油。征用队下午就去了那户人家。Josef拿到一小袋盐和一张优先配给卡，回家时没有走平时那条街。
  _effects: cash 1 (检举邻居得到的配给奖励); reputation 0.020000000000000004; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-7; flag +denounced_neighbor_for_grain_

  _effects: cash -2.2 (两张星期天轻歌剧楼座票); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112; mood 0.06000000000000005; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-112_

  院子里晾着三条床单，风一过就拍到人脸上。Josef把备用钥匙和一张照相馆拍的合影交还，Luise Berg把那件借来的外套叠好递回。两人说好各找各的住处，谁都没有提高声音；分手分得干净，可两个人心里都空了一块。
  _effects: relation_end partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000265:child_

  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang靠窗的桌边，把一只空咖啡杯转了半圈，说房租两人合付、桌子两人共用。Berta Bauer的手停在糖罐盖上，问了一句“要是过不下去呢”，没有当场答应，只说要想一想；杯里的咖啡凉了，话还悬在桌面上。
  _effects: interaction_

- **1944.01** `office16-hire.protege::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/hired-relative` tags: office, hire, patronage, bureaucracy, work, power, vienna, urban
  名单上添的是Franz 2，一个连公文格式都分不清的远房亲戚。Franz Lechner把考勤表放在桌上，没有问原因；Josef也没有解释，只在签字栏里写得比平时慢。第一个月，Franz 2迟到了七次，工资照发。

  Josef在投票前站起来，说今年不选了，让Katharina Kral 2连任，条件是账目每月贴在门口。Katharina Kral 2答应得很快。散会后，几个老团员拍Josef的肩，说“你倒像当家的”。第二个月，账目真的贴在了门口，第一页的墨迹被雨淋得发花。第一页是空白的，第二页才开始有字。
  _effects: mood 0.050000000000000044; reputation 0.04000000000000001; flag +withdrew_from_club_election; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-114_

- **1944.05** `major-crisis.rent-increase/tenant-meeting` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty
  巷口洗衣房的老板娘把几张椅子摆开，叫来半条街的租户。Josef把涨租单摊在桌上，逐条念出上面的数字；Otto Hofer站在门边，既没走，也没替房东说话。大家决定先集体迟交一个月，签名纸传到Josef手里时，纸上已经有一块油渍。

  Maria Weiss没有捂鼻子，反而用撬棍敲了敲棺材底板。声音太实，他又敲了一下；Berta Winter转身就跑，Josef被留在原地。棺材打开时，卷烟码得整整齐齐，白布下面连个人形都没有。Maria Weiss把Josef的名字写在封条背面。

  _effects: health -0.15000000000000002 (postwar illness); cash -4 (medicine); condition_

- **1945.01** `major-family.separation-widowhood/separation-widowhood.separate-smaller-room` tags: family, separation, widowhood, housing, major, lifecycle, household
  Josef把Berta Bauer的礼拜日外套叠好，放在门房。两人去市政办事处问过，办事员说没有哪张表格能写清这种事；神父也不肯在文书上签字。Berta Bauer把铺盖搬到工友的合租房，Josef退掉Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的大房间，在同一栋楼里租了一间后屋，把结婚照留在原来的墙上。
  _effects: household; relation_end partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-27; cash -10 (moving costs and the old room rent); mood -0.07000000000000006_

  军需处先领走了奎宁和碘酒，药房只剩阿司匹林和泻盐。Josef用一双旧靴子换了两支吗啡，药剂师把安瓿裹在报纸里，叮嘱别在街上打开。他把安瓿藏进帽衬，过检查站时低着头。

  Josef排到铁栅门前时，窗口只剩一条缝。Franz Lechner把存折翻到最后一页，说今天每人只兑五十克朗，其余等董事会通知。Katharina 2在后面把存折举过头顶，喊自己存的是金币；Elisabeth Hofer从二楼探出头，只说了一句“都排队”。
  _effects: cash -2 (铁栅门后的第三排); flag +savings_frozen_in_bank_run; knowledge “the bank limits withdrawals to fifty kronen a day”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-36_

  _effects: reputation 0.01999999999999999; skill; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; flag +blocked_permit; knowledge “扩建申请的排水图与消防通道不符合现行规定。”_

  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912; cash -2 (meat and coal for the reconciliation supper); role; flag +family_reconciled; knowledge “两人把煤价、母亲的坟和那笔钱都谈到了。”_

  冬天来得早，煤价涨了，主顾却要压价。Josef守着冷掉的胶锅，把最后一块好木料留给一单可能退掉的活；账本上的欠款又添了一行。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.debt 2 -> 5; cash -1 (workshop winter costs); mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1949.01** `state_tick:wish-craft/craft-pressure/press-on` tags: wish-craft, wish, wish:craft, craft, work, mastery, state, state_tick
  Josef又把那件没实现的事翻出来想了一遍；这一次没有告诉任何人。
  _effects: mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1949.01** `p9-history-public-action.organize-relief/credit-own-name` tags: politics, union, party, activism, public_action, relief, leaflet, task
  夜里 Josef 在灯下重算赊账，Mitzi Lechner 坐在对面搓手，说先顾眼前。锅里剩下的汤已经凝了一层皮，窗外的风把账页吹得哗哗响。
  _effects: cash -5 (以自家名义赊购面粉); food; mood -0.050000000000000044; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1949.09** `r3-war.mia-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::postwar-mia-clarification/found-missing-relative-alive` tags: war, missing, bureaucracy, family, grief, vienna, urban, postwar
  Maria Weiss带来消息，说失踪者可能被俘，战后从西伯利亚回来了，但登记时用了母姓。Josef按地址找去，开门的人少了一只耳朵，盯着Josef看了半天，说“你长得像他”。
  _effects: mood 0.09999999999999998; flag +found_missing_relative_alive; role; knowledge “the missing man returned under his mother’s surname”_

- **1949.11** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.leave` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef看见熟人排在前面，帽檐压得低低的，便转身拐进旁边的小巷。Elisabeth Berg 2追出来问怎么不领了，Josef说人太多，改天再来。回到屋里，灶是冷的，早上剩的半个土豆还在盘子里。Josef把它切成片，就着水咽下去。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; food_

- **1950.01** `office16-favor.private-sinecure::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/sinecure-created` tags: office, favor, corruption, patronage, family, power, vienna, urban
  Josef在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Mitzi Lechner第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Maria Weiss发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @workshop:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:011333; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; flag +private_sinecure; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1950.01** `r13-civ.soup-kitchen::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/pot-ran-dry` tags: social, charity, soup_kitchen, hunger, volunteer, vienna, urban, postwar
  锅底见了铁，队伍还有十几个人。Josef Huber说面粉明天才到，让Josef去门口解释。Josef刚开口，一只空碗就砸在门框上。回家后，他把围裙上的汤渍搓了很久。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (汤锅前排到的第七个人); reputation -0.04999999999999999; flag +soup_kitchen_ran_out_

- **1950.05** `r3-mig.boarding-house::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/accused-of-theft` tags: migration, housing, poverty, lodging, neighbor, vienna, urban, postwar
  Elisabeth Berg 2的怀表不见了，Rosa Kraus把Josef的包袱倒在走廊上。没有表，只有两件衬衣和一块干面包。Elisabeth Berg 2说算了，Rosa Kraus却把Josef的床位从窗边换到门口，说“这样大家都看得见”。
  _effects: mood -0.040000000000000036; condition; flag +accused_of_theft_in_lodging; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000712:child; knowledge “the window bed is the first to be searched”_

- **1950.07** `curse15-betrayal.ledger-share::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/rent-book-rewritten` tags: betrayal, money, family, trust, curse, secret, vienna, urban
  Rosa Winter在房东的租金簿上把Josef的名字划掉，换成自己的，又把两个人合住的房间说成是Rosa Winter一个人租的。房东没有多问，只让Josef月底前搬走。Rosa Winter站在门口，手里拿着那本不该由Rosa Winter保管的簿子。
  _effects: cash -2 (临时找住处与搬家的费用); relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000609:child; reputation -0.03999999999999998; flag +rent_book_taken; condition; mood -0.05999999999999994_

- **1950.09** `office16-scandal.public-hearing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-scandal-helps` tags: office, scandal, media, reputation, politics, power, vienna, urban
  照片登出后，Josef收到十七封邀请函，都是请去讲“如何面对舆论”。一个工商业协会送来一笔“研究资助”，请Josef去讲一次闭门课；课酬刚好补上办公室这个月的缺口。
  _effects: reputation 0.04999999999999999; organization_funds; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:434; flag +scandal_turned_into_speaking_fee; knowledge “十七封邀请函里，有十二封问的是同一件事：能不能把丑闻讲成经验。”; mood 0.040000000000000036; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1951.01** `office16-admin.quiet-ledger::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-ledger-was-cooked` tags: office, administration, budget, competence, quiet, power, vienna, urban
  前任留下的账本太整齐了：每一笔都落在同一个日期，连墨水颜色都一样。Josef让Gustav Bauer 2抽了三页去核对，发现其中两页的收据号码连续，却来自两家不同的商号。Gustav Bauer 2合上本子，问要不要现在报上去。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; state_add office-under-siege state:world-vienna-1912:448; knowledge “前任账本里两页收据号码连续，却来自两家不同商号。”; flag -office_under_siege; flag +office_under_siege_

- **1951.01** `state_tick:wc17-master-craftsperson/workshop-year/commissions-arrive` tags: wc17-master-craftsperson, craft, work, master, workshop, ownership, reputation, state
  Josef的作坊又接了两单：一扇橡木门、一只修到一半的八音盒。Josef把工钱先付给学徒，自己留下最后一张钞票，用铅笔在账本背面记下欠料行的数目。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 5 -> 7; cash 2 (workshop commissions); reputation 0.020000000000000018_

- **1951.01** `p9-work-jobsearch.ask-trial/ask-trial.no-vacancy` tags: work, job_search, negotiation, wages, employment, task, trial, factory
  工头说眼下没有空缺，让他把名字和住址写在账本撕下的纸角上，说月底再来问。Josef看那张纸被随手压进抽屉，纸角还露出半截，知道多半不会有人翻。他道过谢，往街尾另一家走去。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-4; flag +seeking_work; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-149_

- **1951.04** `vienna-church-community.tenement-prayer/rent-prayer-help` tags: faith, social, family, home
  楼梯间只点一根蜡烛。Mitzi Lechner凑过来低声说，楼下老寡妇的喘病又重了，教区明早才能派修女来。Josef把汤碗接过去，碗底还温着。
  _effects: condition; food; relation acquaintance with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1951.11** `r1-civ.mountain-border-crossing::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/crossed-mountain-border` tags: migration, border, smuggling, patrol, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  向导带着Josef走山路，雪到小腿，界桩上刷的白漆已经剥落。Elisabeth Berg 2说过了前面那道铁丝网就是另一个国家，巡逻狗每半小时过一趟。Josef把行李绑在背上，走到铁丝网前，才发现下面被剪开的口子又被人用铁丝拧上了。
  _effects: cash -6 (山口的界桩); health -0.09999999999999998 (山口的界桩); condition; flag +crossed_mountain_border; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; knowledge “the cut in the wire is repaired every few days”_

- **1952.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Alois Weiss 2在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Alois Weiss 2在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-156_

- **1952.01** `office16-rival.removal-motion::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/the-vote-ties` tags: office, rivalry, politics, removal, power, intrigue, vienna, urban
  第二轮投票是十七比十七。按章程，主席可以投第二票；Josef把自己的名字念了两遍，会议室里没有人笑。散会后，Luise Lechner在门口说下一次会带更多的人来，Josef说椅子够用。
  _effects: reputation -0.020000000000000018; mood -0.020000000000000018; relation rival with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000587:child; flag +tie_broken_by_chair; knowledge “章程允许主席在平票时投第二票，但没有人记得上一次使用是什么时候。”_

- **1952.01** `vienna-parish-aid.funeral-society/funeral-society.defer` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Josef把抽屉拉开又推上，铜币在木板上滚了半圈。Rosa Kraus合上本子说，拖欠的人不少，可规矩就是规矩。她走下两级台阶又回头提醒，发工钱那天她会在教堂侧门等着。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000712:child_

  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

- **1952.09** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.lost_room_key/key_stolen_by_stranger` tags: home, social, family, housing
  Josef记得出门时钥匙还在，回来却没了。Gustav Weiss说下午看见一个穿灰外套的男人在楼道里转悠，手里晃着一把铜钥匙。两人下楼查看，房门虚掩，抽屉被拉开，攒着买煤的六克朗不见了。Gustav Weiss陪着Josef去街角警察岗亭报了案，警察记下灰外套，说会留意。
  _effects: cash -6 (被盗的买煤钱); relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004823:child_

  有人拿走了Josef画在牛皮纸上的榫卯图样，几周后，同样的柜子出现在另一条街的橱窗里，标价更低。Josef没有去告，只在自家门框上重描了一遍印记。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.backlog 7 -> 5; reputation -0.020000000000000018_

  Josef给Gustav Bauer 2安排了一个“礼仪顾问”的位置：有办公室、有名牌、有固定工资，没有任何需要签字的文件。Gustav Bauer 2接受了，并且在第一次会议上坐到了离主席最远的那把椅子上。

- **1953.01** `major-crisis.rent-increase/pay-the-increase` tags: crisis, housing, rent, eviction, family, poverty, injury
  房东没有露面，只让看门人Elisabeth Berg 2把补款单塞进门缝：每月要多十四克朗，先交十八克朗（本月十四加四克朗手续费）才肯谈续租。Josef把准备买冬煤的钱数了两遍，还是把钱交了，收据上写的是“自愿捐助”。Elisabeth Berg 2低声说，不签的人已经有两家搬走了，楼下的煤价牌又换了数字。
  _effects: cash -18 (advance payment demanded with the rent increase); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; flag +rent_increase_accepted; health -0.020000000000000018 (winter coal money spent on the rent increase)_

  走到第四家，Josef的帽子还是轻的。有人说明天再给，有人说自家也刚办过丧事。Josef把帽子里的钱倒在Elisabeth Berg 2的桌上，数了两遍，还差一口棺材的钱；Elisabeth Berg 2说算了，用旧门板钉一个也行。
  _effects: cash -1 (端着一顶帽子走楼道); flag +funeral_collection_short; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; knowledge “a plain coffin costs more than the building can raise”; condition_

- **1953.06** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef没有立刻答话，先去敲Rosa Kraus的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Rosa Kraus提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Josef把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000712:child; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute_

  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004518:child; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  冬天来得早，煤价涨了，主顾却要压价。Josef守着冷掉的胶锅，把最后一块好木料留给一单可能退掉的活；账本上的欠款又添了一行。
  _effects: state_parameter wc17-master-craftsperson.debt 5 -> 8; cash -1 (workshop winter costs)_

- **1955.01** `r13-work.mentor-worker::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-factory/said-nothing` tags: work, mentoring, safety, solidarity, youth, vienna, urban, postwar
  午休时Maria Bauer坐在对面，Josef看见Josef的手腕上有一道新划痕，想问，又觉得不关自己的事。Luise Kral来催下午的活，Maria Bauer把饭盒一收就进了车间。Josef后来听说Maria Bauer那天下午被皮带擦伤了胳膊，伤得不重，但请了三天假。
  _effects: flag +work13_stayed_silent_with_young_worker; knowledge “the young worker was injured at the belt a week later”_

- **1955.11** `r1-hc.sex-work::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement::sex-work-reform-laundry-board/sex-work-reform-hired` tags: street, sex_work, rent, police, survival, vienna, urban, postwar
  Josef排在队伍里，前面的女人一直低着头。招工的人只问会不会用熨斗，Josef说会；当天就上工，午饭是一碗扁豆汤。晚上回家，Josef把工牌放在桌上，房东看了很久。
  _effects: cash 3 (后楼梯间的租钱); flag +hired_at_municipal_laundry; role; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:005945:child; knowledge “the laundry asks only whether the applicant can iron”; employment 熨衣工 @ org-winter-print_

- **1956.01** `vienna-family-same-sex-male/private-arrangement` tags: family, intimacy, same_sex, persecution, lifecycle, partnership
  Josef和Friedrich Lechner在城边租了一间小屋，对外只说是同事合住。房租四克朗，两人不同时出门，信件用假名，夜里把窗帘拉严；Friedrich Lechner在门后放了一根木棍，Josef把两人的工票分开放，连咳嗽都压着声。
  _effects: relation partner with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:007265:child; mood 0.040000000000000036; cash -4 (separate room rent)_

- **1956.01** `office16-budget.sign-or-block::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/signed-with-margin` tags: office, budget, permit, administration, power, economy, vienna, urban
  Josef在预算表上签了字，把冬季煤房的款项从三笔并成一笔，又给夜班守卫加了半份津贴。Berta 2算出省下的钱刚好够修屋顶；没有人鼓掌，但一月的会议室不再需要穿大衣。
  _effects: reputation 0.03999999999999998; skill; mood 0.020000000000000018; knowledge “把冬季煤房的款项并成一笔后，省下的钱够修会议室屋顶。”_

- **1956.01** `agency:organize_meeting` tags: agency, agency:organize_meeting
  Josef原定在Berggasse 19, Wohnung und Ordination开会，但来的人不够，或者有人临时退缩；最后只留下几张没发完的纸条。
  _effects: reputation -0.010000000000000009_

  厂子没有关，但工钱减了三成，工时加了一个钟头。Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne说这是暂时的。没有人相信“暂时”，但也没有人当场走。
  _effects: cash -2 (车间门口的封条); mood -0.06000000000000005; state_add strike-wave state:world-vienna-1912:553_

- **1956.09** `r3-mig.refugee-aid-society::ctx-vienna-postwar-1945::milieu-tenement/soup-and-a-coat` tags: migration, relief, organization, charity, risk, vienna, urban, postwar
  救济会的地下室里烧着一锅豆汤，Gustav Bauer给Josef盛了一碗，又在名册上添了一个名字。角落里堆着捐来的大衣，Josef挑了一件缺扣子的。Gustav Bauer说“别挑，明天可能就没了”。
  _effects: food; flag +received_aid_society_help; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-61; organization_membership; knowledge “the society serves soup until the pot is empty”_

  名单上添的是Gustav Weiss，一个连公文格式都分不清的远房亲戚。Gustav Bauer 2把考勤表放在桌上，没有问原因；Josef也没有解释，只在签字栏里写得比平时慢。第一个月，Gustav Weiss迟到了七次，工资照发。

  Josef提醒Anna Winter别替Gustav Bauer私下搬料，Anna Winter却把这话学给了Gustav Bauer听。下午Gustav Bauer把Josef叫到办公室，问是不是在拉帮结派。Josef说是教新人规矩，Gustav Bauer让他少管闲事；走出办公室，Anna Winter正在擦机器，头也没抬。

  _effects: cash -0.3 (缴纳丧葬互助会的月钱); reputation 0.03999999999999998_

  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (手掌被滚烫铅字烫伤); cash 1.2 (当日未扣工的计件工资); reputation -0.06_

  Josef在编制表最下面添了一行“顾问”，月薪两克朗，职责是“必要时提供咨询”。Gustav Weiss第二天就搬来一张靠窗的桌子，带来一盆天竺葵；一个月后，Lena发现顾问唯一经手的文件是自己的午餐菜单。
  _effects: position 顾问 @workshop:person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912-evt:world-vienna-1912:011333; organization_funds; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004823:child; knowledge “顾问岗位没有固定职责，考勤由我签字。”; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Maria Weiss 的话很简短：名单不能批。Josef 把表格折好，转头去搬砖搭灶。Hermann Lechner 4 从家里抱来一捆柴，火星溅在围裙上，拍两下就灭了。
  _effects: cash -3 (买柴火和盐); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-115_

  Berta 2把藤箱倒扣在长桌上，衣服、照片和一只铁皮饭盒滚出来。他拆开母亲缝在衬里的信，念了两句就放下，说“这种信不能带”。Gustav Bauer在旁边把衣服一件件叠回去，叠得比原来整齐。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; flag +luggage_inspected; knowledge “the inspector reads letters sewn into luggage linings”_

  师傅作坊把Josef叫进办公室，桌上摊着两张排班表，说老工头下个月退休，问Josef敢不敢接。Josef接过表，看见自己那一栏被划掉，换成了“工头”。走出门时，Anna Winter正在擦机器，抬头看了一眼，又低下去。

  Josef没有只拿订单，而是跟Elisabeth Berg 2谈成每月固定送一版价目表和两百张单据，月底结账。Emil Huber替双方写了张简单的字据，写明纸价涨跌另算。Josef把字据和名片夹放在一起，回作坊时脚步比去时轻。
  _effects: cash 12 (月度印刷活的第一笔预付款); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-90; role; flag +retainer_client; knowledge “月度印刷合同要把纸价涨跌单独写明。”_

- **1959.04** `vienna-parish-aid.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.take-and-carry` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  汤是豆子熬的，表面浮着几粒油星，气味把整条巷子都占了。Berta Moser 2排在前面，回头替Josef看着位置，嘴里数着还剩几个人。铁罐递过去时烫手，Josef用围裙角垫着。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120; mood 0.030000000000000027; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-120_

- **1959.08** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.river-walk/river-walk.far` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, walk, river, injury
  Josef顺着河岸一直走，走过铁路桥又走回来，天已经黑透。回来时脚底发热，袜子磨在脚背上，她一路数着路灯走完最后一段巷子，进门先坐下脱了鞋。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (走了很远的碎石路，脚底发疼); mood 0.050000000000000044_

  Josef在单间里站了一刻钟，脚底发潮，墙纸底下透出一股霉味。看门人说刷一层石灰就好，Anna Stein 2却在门口轻轻咳了一声，提醒这背面没有阳光。Josef把定金收回口袋，谢过看门人，宁可多付三克朗，也不愿把湿气带进夜里。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140; flag +still_looking_for_room; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-140_

- **1960.01** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-pay` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Josef把通知念给Maria Winter听，Maria Winter摇头说是楼梯间那几块新玻璃的账。Josef去楼下杂货铺换开一张五克朗的纸币，回屋一枚一枚摆开数，煤箱底多留了二十赫勒。
  _effects: cash -2 (按月多付两克朗房租); food; mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1960.01** `p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club/p9-social-daily-culture.singing-club.ask-fare` tags: daily, culture, leisure, cafe, music, walk, task, task:social
  Josef向Mitzi Weiss问清下个月郊外演唱会的车费由谁出，Mitzi Weiss说团里只贴一半，另一半要团员自己凑。Josef把四十赫勒硬币在指间转了两圈，最终没有放进收款盒，说等发了工钱再来；这一来一去，也算清了半票、酒棚和车钱各是多少。出门时Elisabeth Berg 2正扛着啤酒桶进后厅，两人侧身让过。
  _effects: mood -0.020000000000000018; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:004518:child; skill_

- **1960.03** `vienna-market-household.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.sell` tags: home, market, food, social
  鞋匠敲后门问有没有热汤，Josef把剩下的半锅倒进他的罐子里，收下两个十字币。Rosa Weiss提着空碗上来时，只看见Josef把硬币塞进围裙口袋。
  _effects: cash 2 (卖出半锅扁豆汤); food_

- **1960.05** `statistical_infection`
  Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺结核让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1960.06** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Josef的肺结核没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1960.08** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Friedrich Winter用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Josef才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000299:child; flag +parish_registered_

- **1960.11** `vienna-tenement-neighbors.sick_child_doorstep/helps_fetch_doctor` tags: home, social, family, housing, injury
  隔壁Rosa Lechner的小儿子烧到说胡话，额头烫得能烘手。Rosa Lechner在楼道里抹眼泪，说请不起医生。Josef下楼叫了街角药房的伙计，花四十赫勒买了一包退烧药粉，又帮着用湿布敷了一整夜。天亮时孩子退了烧，Rosa Lechner端来一碗热汤面，面底下卧着一个鸡蛋。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (买退烧药粉); health -0.020000000000000018 (熬夜照料病人); relation friend with person-rosa-lechner_

- **1960.12** `statistical_death/tuberculosis` tags: death
  Josef咳了几个月，手帕上开始带血。1960年冬天，Josef在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的床上消瘦下去，再没有力气去上工。Mitzi Winter 2在场。
  _effects: death (肺结核)_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.mind-sibling/sibling-falls-at-well` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, sibling, care
  Maria Winter的鞋掉在井台边，Rosa先去捡鞋，回头才看见Maria Winter摔在湿石头上。Alois Huber用围裙裹住Maria Winter，血从膝盖渗出来。Alois Lechner夜里把Rosa叫到灶台前，数落了很久，最后把锅里剩下的半勺汤倒回大锅，只给Rosa留了半碗。
  _effects: health -0.030000000000000027 (在井台边摔了一跤); mood -0.06; relation kin with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-16_

- **1918.09** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Berta Weiss 2的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Rosa坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Berta Weiss 2把桶一放，夹起Rosa进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Alois Lechner赶来时，Rosa正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food; relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1918.09** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/vinegar-compress` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  碗里倒上醋，兑一半凉水，Alois Lechner把布巾浸透，拧到不滴水，敷在Rosa的额头上。布一热就换，换下来的搭在床沿。Rosa半夜说梦话，Alois Lechner用手背试了试温度，又去换一盆水。天亮时枕布湿了一片，Alois Lechner的手指白得像泡过石灰。
  _effects: health 0.030000000000000027 (湿敷后退了烧); mood 0.02; relation kin with person-alois-lechner_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  “卖完了。”三个字从窗口里传出来，Rosa后面的队伍立刻散了一半。Rosa没走，趴在窗台上看伙计扫面屑，直到人家挥手赶人。旁边摊子的土豆带着泥，Rosa用围裙兜住，一枚一枚数铜板，回家报账时连找零都说清了。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  “拽平，别留褶。”Josef Bauer在绳子那头喊。Rosa踮着脚把床单拉直，碱水把指缝泡得发白。收工时天已经黑了，Josef Bauer数了三赫勒，又在围裙上擦干手才递过来。Rosa把钱塞进袜筒，回家路上一直攥着，虎口的裂口沾了汗，火辣辣地疼。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); health -0.030000000000000027 (碱水泡手，虎口裂开); relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer_

  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (膝盖在湿石板上磕破); relation acquaintance with person-anna-moser_

  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息（其中0.02克朗记入债务）); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-25_

  Rosa被推到拱廊柱子边，围裙带子绕了两圈，系在铁环上。Rosa一挣，肩膀撞上石柱，整条胳膊麻了一下。Lena提着菜篮过来，把篮子往地上一放，从怀里摸出削土豆的小刀，挑开绳结，没跟那两个孩子说一句话。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.04999999999999999; condition_

  Josef Bauer把Rosa拽到队伍前头，往自己身前塞，说“孩子占不了多大地儿”。窗口一次递出两包面包，Rosa把邻居那包夹在胳肢窝，自己这包抱在胸前。到Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang门口，Rosa蹲下把两包并排，按Josef Bauer说的份量分开，连找零的铜板也分成两小堆。
  _effects: food; relation acquaintance with person-josef-bauer; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

  _effects: mood -0.07999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-karl-kraus_

  _effects: cash -1.2 (把攒下的零钱塞给离家的兄姐（其中1.2克朗记入债务）); relation kin with person-franz-lechner; mood -0.03_

  Rosa没有进厂，也没有留在家里，而是搭了去乡下的火车。姨妈家在葡萄园边上，缺人摘果子。Rosa把课本塞进箱底，白天干活，晚上在油灯下认几个字。秋天寄回家的信里，Rosa说这里的星星比城里多。车票两克朗，是姨妈先垫的。
  _effects: cash -2 (书包挂在门后的钉子上); flag +left_school_for_country; move place-klosterneuburg-aunt-house_

  _effects: health -0.11999999999999998 (hand caught in press); condition; cash -4 (hospital transport); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

  _effects: skill; reputation 0.04; mood 0.04999999999999999; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-57_

  Rosa没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth); condition_

  面包店门口的台阶上还留着扫过的面粉印。Rosa把话说得很慢，怕对方听错；Josef Hofer 2没有立刻答，先问清几点、走哪条路、妹妹能不能同行，然后点了头。

  _effects: mood -0.010000000000000009_

- **1930.07** `vienna-illness-household.child-fever/call-doctor` tags: health, family, home
  夜里Grete Winter哭醒，Rosa独自用湿布敷额，熬到街上有人扫雪才去请医生。医生进门时靴底带着雪泥，听完胸口说无大碍；那三克朗花得心疼，却换回一条稳下来的呼吸。
  _effects: health 0.09999999999999998 (医生诊治后退烧); cash -3 (支付医生诊费和药粉); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

  炉门一开，黑烟先扑出来，Rosa眯着眼把纸团往里塞。火苗舔着碎煤慢慢亮起来，Otto Winter隔着门喊当心一氧化碳。水壶开始咕噜作响，屋里才有点热乎气。
  _effects: cash -0.6 (购买一小筐碎煤); health -0.010000000000000009 (生火时被烟呛到); food_

- **1931.10** `kernel-crisis.task-rescue-neighbor::ctx-klosterneuburg-rural-1912::milieu-tenement/rescue-too-late` tags: task, rescue, fire, neighbor, betrayal, rural, agriculture, small_town
  Rosa撞开阁楼门，烟先滚出来，Ferdinand Huber倒在门后，手指还扣着门槛。Rosa和Grete Lechner 2拽着Ferdinand Huber的腋下往外拖，拖到楼梯口时人已经没气；梁木跟着塌下，只压住一只烧焦的箱子。消防队赶开围观的人，登记了Rosa的名字，Ferdinand Huber的名字写在另一张纸上。Rosa的袖口烧穿，去诊所包扎花了三克朗，那只箱子留在院子里，谁也没再碰。
  _effects: death (smoke inhalation and burns in a building fire); health -0.1 (smoke inhalation while searching the burning room); condition; role; cash -3 (burnt clothes and a doctor visit); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-59_

- **1931.11** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa和Josef谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Rosa家，钥匙只在白天给。Josef说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Anton Winter 2听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Rosa把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Josef-world-vienna-1912; mood 0.020000000000000018_

  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne在事故后第三天通知Rosa不用再来了，说岗位不能一直空着。Rosa拿着医院证明去工会，诊断书被工头压在抽屉里；治伤花去六克朗，Rosa的手还没好，工作先没了，工牌交回去时手指还缠着纱布。
  _effects: unemployment (dismissed after accident); health -0.12 (injury and lost job); cash -6 (medical treatment)_

  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; skill; mood 0.07; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32_

- **1932.04** `p9-gap-adult-solitude.leaking-shoe/leaking-shoe.gave-up` tags: adulthood, daily, solitude, ordinary, home, repair, shoe, poverty
  Rosa缝了半个钟头，线老是从针眼里脱出来，最后把鞋往床底下一踢。第二天清早她穿着这双鞋站在厂门口等开门，湿石头地的凉气从裂缝里钻进来，脚趾冻得发麻，只能来回换脚。
  _effects: health -0.05 (穿着漏水的鞋在冷地上站久了); mood -0.040000000000000036_

- **1933.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/home-care` tags: health, work, family, injury
  Rosa没有去诊所，只用热水和旧布洗了伤口。第二天伤口周围发红，家里人把仅剩的干净床单撕成条。
  _effects: health -0.03 (untreated wound); cash -2 (clean cloth)_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004734_

- **1933.01** `vienna-parish-aid.rent-box/rent-box.accept-all` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  煤袋落在台阶上，扬起细细一层黑末。Otto Stein用铅笔头在蓝格册上写门牌号，问房东姓什么，又叮嘱别把面包票弄丢。灶上水壶开始叫，Rosa才想起屋里连只干净的杯子都没有。
  _effects: cash 6 (互助会补上本月房租差额); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13; flag +parish_registered_

- **1933.01** `p9-social-relationship-initiative.apology-at-bakery/settle-the-account` tags: courtship, friendship, family, social, initiative, housing, task, task:social
  七点刚过，Josef Weiss照例来买一只 Semmel。Rosa把紫罗兰和两只 Semmel 放在柜台上，先付了四十赫勒，又把上回算错的账一笔一笔说清：借出去的是八克朗，记成了十克朗，是自己记错了。Josef Weiss听完没马上说话，把零钱数进围裙口袋，最后说中午过来喝碗汤。
  _effects: cash -0.4 (一小束紫罗兰和两只 Semmel); relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-32; mood 0.08000000000000002_

- **1934.01** `r13-work.factory-gate::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/turned-away` tags: work, hiring, factory, queue, cash, vienna, urban, crisis
  Josef Hofer把名单卷起来，说今天只要会看图纸的。Rosa在铁门外站到换班，听见里面皮带轮转了一整天。Anna Winter 2分给Rosa半块黑面包，两人谁也没提明天还来不来；电车票钱花掉了，口袋里的铜板只够买一小袋煤。
  _effects: cash -1 (铁门开了一条缝); mood -0.12; flag +work13_turned_away_at_factory_gate; knowledge “the factory takes only ten men from a gate queue of over a h”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-71_

- **1934.02** `r3-mig.remittance-letter::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/remittance-returned-address` tags: migration, remittance, family, post, money, vienna, urban, crisis
  Franz Huber把汇款单退回来，说收款村的地址在新区划里改了名，钱只能退回。Rosa又填了一张，把旧村名和新村名并排写上。Franz Huber说这样不行，系统只认一个名字。汇款单在柜台上放了一下午，最后被盖上“无法投递”。
  _effects: cash -1 (汇款单背面的三行字); mood -0.09999999999999998; condition; flag +remittance_address_failed; knowledge “the post office accepts only one official village name”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1934.04** `kernel-health.street-violence::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/street-violence-beaten` tags: health, violence, street, injury, death, vienna, urban, crisis
  巷口的争吵从一句脏话变成拳头。Rosa被推倒在排水沟边，Helene Huber的鞋跟踩在肋侧；等巡警吹哨，打人的那伙人已经散了，只剩Rosa的一颗牙在雪里。他把那颗牙包在手帕里，第二天才想起去诊所。
  _effects: health -0.12 (kicked in the ribs during a street fight); condition; role; cash -5 (doctor, bandages and a tram home); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63; knowledge “巷口打架时巡警要等哨声才来，打人的那伙人总先散，牙掉了只能自己捡。”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-63_

- **1934.06** `r13-turn.pregnancy::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::war-food-shortage-pregnancy/miscarriage` tags: family, pregnancy, marriage, household, risk, vienna, urban, crisis
  第五周夜里，Rosa被腹部的坠痛惊醒，走到院子里时，血已经顺着腿流下来。Josef Hofer去叫了接生婆，接生婆看了一眼，说月份太早，留不住。Friedrich Winter天亮才回来，Rosa把染红的布藏在洗衣盆底下，说只是来早了。
  _effects: health -0.10000000000000019 (洗衣盆边的验孕布); mood -0.20000000000000007; condition; flag +pregnancy_lost_early; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-34_

- **1934.09** `vienna-housing-move.rent-notice/rent-notice-demand-repair` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa没有立刻答话，先去敲Franz Winter 2的门。两人一起数落墙上的水渍和门框里灌进来的风，楼梯间的窗户修好了，自家墙皮还在剥落。Franz Winter 2提议一起把话带给看门人：先修好漏水的墙角，涨租的事再谈。Rosa把纸重新折好，请他把话带到门外。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74; mood 0.030000000000000027; flag +rent_dispute; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-74_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-divorce/reconcile-for-now` tags: family, marriage, divorce, housing, lifecycle
  Rosa和Friedrich Winter在楼道里吵到半夜，最后因为下个月的房租又坐回同一张桌。谁都没有提和好，只把账本重新摊开；Friedrich Winter把煤钱推过来，Rosa接过去数了两遍，手指碰到一起又分开，屋里的灯亮到很晚。
  _effects: mood -0.030000000000000027_

- **1935.01** `vienna-family-childbirth/son-born` tags: family, childbirth, death, lifecycle, birth, injury, state, state:raising-child
  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Emil Winter。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Friedrich Winter把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Rosa躺在床上出了一身虚汗。
  _effects: birth male; health -0.08 (childbirth); cash -6 (midwife fee); state_add raising-child state:world-vienna-1912:181_

- **1935.01** `r13-edu.correspondence-course::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/cannot-read-lessons` tags: education, correspondence, adult_education, literacy, mail, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa读到第三课就跟不上，题目里的“比例”和“折旧”都认不全。Rosa把讲义压在箱底，直到邮差来问续费，才发现已经欠了三期。钱退不回来，Rosa把没拆的信封当柴烧了。第二年，Rosa在厂里听人念夜校的招生简章，才知道那门课还在办。
  _effects: cash -3 (邮包里的第七课); mood -0.06999999999999995_

- **1935.02** `vienna-housing-move.sublet-room/sublet-daylight-only` tags: housing, home, rent, family, money
  Rosa和Berta Weiss 2谈妥只出租白天，夜里不留宿，收两克朗。床仍归Rosa家，钥匙只在白天给。Berta Weiss 2说不碍事，他住的工棚不许带人，白天有个落脚的地方就够。Elisabeth Winter听了点头，说这样少些闲话；到了傍晚，Rosa把床铺重新收好，屋里照旧几口人。
  _effects: cash 2 (白天出租床位的钱); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10; mood 0.020000000000000018_

- **1935.07** `r2-law.rent-contract::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/sublet-a-room` tags: law, rent, contract, housing, tenement, vienna, urban, crisis
  Rosa把靠街的小房间转租给Anna Hofer的表弟，租金正好补上涨幅。Josef Weiss 2来查房时看见多了一张床，问合同里有没有写转租；Rosa说第十三条只写修房，没写住人。Josef Weiss 2在门口站了一会儿，说下个月加两克朗。
  _effects: cash 7 (租约上的第十三条); flag +sublet_room_without_landlord_consent; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53; knowledge “the landlord will raise rent for the extra lodger”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-53_

- **1936.01** `vienna-life-injury-recovery/clinic-treatment` tags: health, work, family
  Rosa在受伤后的第三天去了诊所。医生清洗伤口、换药，收下诊费，嘱咐不要急着回机器旁。Rosa把药方折好，回家路上买了纱布。
  _effects: health 0.04999999999999999 (wound treatment); cash -8 (clinic and bandages); condition_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004914_

  Rosa在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang生下一个男婴，取名Emil Winter。工友凑的钱抵了一部分，家里还是从糖罐里数出六克朗给接生的人；Friedrich Winter把消息带到工厂，回来时把唯一的摇篮重新刷了一遍，Rosa躺在床上出了一身虚汗。

- **1936.01** `r13-fam.arrange-childcare::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement::childcare-war/creche-full` tags: task, care, childcare, work, neighbor, wages, vienna, urban
  托儿所的名额满了，管事的让Rosa把孩子送到城另一头的教会收容所。Rosa算了一趟车钱和路上的时辰，知道赶不上早班。Anna Moser 2在门口听见，说可以替Rosa看半天，另一半时间让邻居轮着来；Rosa把孩子的名字写在纸条上，贴在自己工牌背面。
  _effects: cash -1 (上工前把小的送下楼); condition; flag +shared_childcare_with_neighbours; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “the neighbours will take turns watching the child”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1936.07** `vienna-women-housework.wash-day/wash-day.rush` tags: gender, housework, home, labour, family, injury
  木盆边的水渍映着天光，Rosa跪在石阶上揉搓床单，肘弯发酸。晾绳上的湿布滴答作响，Elisabeth Winter探身把自家被单也挂上去，两人没多说话。
  _effects: health -0.01999999999999999 (长时间浸在碱水里，手部皮肤开裂); relation acquaintance with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000303:child_

- **1936.08** `vienna-medical-treatment.night-fever/corner-doctor` tags: health, medical, family, money
  药粉含在嘴里发苦，Rosa就着糖浆咽下去，喉头一阵凉。医生拎包出门时，楼梯间的煤气灯刚好灭了，Berta Weiss摸黑送他到拐角，回来时手里攥着找回的几枚赫勒。
  _effects: cash -4 (医生出诊费和药费); health 0.04999999999999999 (药粉和糖浆缓解症状); relation friend with person-berta-weiss_

- **1936.12** `r3-war.liberation-flag::ctx-vienna-authoritarian-1934::milieu-tenement/raised-flag-on-liberation-day` tags: war, liberation, occupation, flag, aftermath, vienna, urban, crisis
  第一面旗挂反了，蓝边朝上，Maria Huber在梯子上骂了一句，Rosa在下面扶着梯子笑。街角还有零星的枪声，Luise Berg把藏了三年的葡萄酒搬出来，瓶塞一开，泡沫喷了Rosa一脸。Franz Lechner 2带着人从教堂那边过来，说“先别庆祝，仓库还在烧”。
  _effects: mood 0.16000000000000003; flag +raised_flag_on_liberation_day; relation friend with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation friend with person:evt:world-vienna-1912:000265:child; knowledge “the depot is still burning”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-81; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-85_

- **1936.12** `statistical_death/childbirth` tags: death
  Rosa生产后一直发热，腹部疼痛。接生的人说已经排干净了，但几天后高热不退，Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang的家里人连夜去请医生，仍没赶上。Berta Weiss 2在场。
  _effects: death (产后感染与出血)_

### 7 subject events

- **1913.02** `vienna-infant-household.laundry-day/sibling-watches` tags: infancy, health, family, home
  Josef Bauer在厨房的木盆里搓亚麻布，热水汽模糊了窗。Anna Bauer坐在摇床边的矮凳上晃拨浪鼓，Emil伸手去抓。等Josef Bauer晾完最后一块尿布，Emil已经在Anna Bauer的看护下睡熟，摇床没被蹬歪。
  _effects: relation kin with person-phase15-Emil-world-vienna-1912; health 0.020000000000000018 (被看护着，没磕碰); mood 0.03_

- **1915.01** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/neighbor-carries` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  Anna Moser的煤桶磕在楼梯转角，煤块滚了一地。Emil坐在平台上，膝盖渗出血珠。Anna Moser把桶一放，夹起Emil进了自家门，用温水冲伤口，又拿一块干面包塞过来。Josef Bauer赶来时，Emil正坐在厨房凳上啃面包。
  _effects: health -0.020000000000000018 (膝盖擦破，已冲洗); food_

- **1917.04** `vienna-childhood-street.hoop/hoop-mother-call` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  窗户上的玻璃响了一下，有人探出头，让Emil立刻回家。铁环还在Mitzi Lechner脚边，Emil蹲下去抽，抽不动。Mitzi Lechner说除非明天把铁钩带来。Emil点头，Mitzi Lechner把脚一抬，铁环滚到Emil手边，上面沾了一层鞋底灰。
  _effects: relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner; mood -0.02_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Emil拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Gustav Moser弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Emil接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-2; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-13_

- **1918.06** `vienna-early-childhood.stairwell-fall/arm-bruise` tags: childhood, family, home, street, injury
  黑楼梯间只有门缝透进来的一点光。Emil踩空时伸手去抓扶手，手臂先撞在铁端上，整个人滚下三级。Josef Bauer把Emil抱到灶边，拿湿冷毛巾按住肿起的地方，又撕了一条旧布缠紧。第二天Emil举着胳膊下楼，照样去院子里玩。
  _effects: health -0.040000000000000036 (从楼梯滚下，小臂挫伤); mood -0.05000000000000002_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-sold-out-potatoes` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  木窗“咔”地合上，Emil的布袋还敞着口。伙计隔着板缝说下星期再来。Emil在墙根站了一会儿，把硬币排在掌心数了两遍，走到旁边土豆摊前，挑了几个小的，让摊主当面过秤。家里人晚上把钱和土豆倒在桌上，重新数了一遍。
  _effects: food; mood -0.03; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-10_

- **1920.01** `statistical_death/violence` tags: death
  Emil在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang卷入一场冲突，头部和胸口受了重伤。旁人把人抬回家，夜里伤势恶化。Elisabeth Bauer在场。
  _effects: death (暴力)_

  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1917.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

  Lena熬过了这场肺炎，能坐起来喝一点汤，但身体还很虚。

- **1918.01** `vienna-life-school-enrolment/half-day-place` tags: childhood, school, fees
  学校只收半日班，Emil Hofer说下午还有空位，但课本要家里自己买。Josefine Hofer先交了一克朗，把Lena的名字写在候补名单上。
  _effects: education primary; cash -1 (partial school fee（其中1克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-borrowed-from-teacher` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  “借你可以，拿劳动换。”Emil Hofer把旧石板推到桌沿，又指了指墙角的灰桶。Lena先去后院倒灰，风把炉灰吹回来，落在睫毛上。回来时Emil Hofer已经用粉笔在石板背面写了一个“借”字，说擦掉那天就是还石板的日子。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; health -0.009999999999999995 (每天多留倒炉灰，沾上粉尘)_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/recites` tags: childhood, school, hunger
  Grete Stein让Lena站到黑板前背乘法表。孩子背到七乘八时停了一下，还是背完了；放学时手指冻得发红，石板上的字被袖子擦掉一半，Grete Stein在名册上给了一个小小的勾，Lena把石板抱在胸前，一路没舍得放进书包。
  _effects: education primary_

- **1919.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/attends-through-frost` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Lena踩着结冰的台阶进教室，第三个空位还空着。Emil Hofer让Lena把昨天的课文背完，又用戒尺敲了敲黑板，叫全班跟着念。放学时名字被写在红榜末尾，Lena把冻僵的手指揣进袖口，觉得明天还能来。放学路上，Lena把冻裂的手贴在热煤渣上。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +school_attendance_streak; skill_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.fetch-coal/coal-neighbor-speaks` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, coal, errand
  掌柜不肯赊，Alois Kral从队尾挤过来，说这户人家的男人在印刷所做了十年，欠不了一筐煤。掌柜把“两赫勒”写在门牌号旁边，煤只装了小半筐。Lena抱着筐经过Alois Kral身边，Alois Kral替Lena把绳结紧了紧。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (赊账添的两赫勒利息); food; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-18_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/runs-errands` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Lena放学后替Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne的排字工送校样，穿过两条街，拿到一克朗。回家时天已经黑了，Josefine Hofer把饭留在锅里；Lena的鞋底磨薄，脚后跟起了泡，却把那一克朗捏得平平整整，放进Josefine Hofer的围裙口袋；这跑腿的差事从此固定下来，Lena也算家里一个能挣钱的孩子。
  _effects: cash 1 (running errands); role_

- **1921.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.market-line/line-half-flour` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, market, food
  面粉袋压在胸口，越抱越沉。Lena前面的大婶一直往后靠，Lena就把膝盖顶住对方的篮子，硬留出半步。买到半袋面粉后，Lena的鞋跟留在人堆里，找回来时鞋面全是泥。回家路上布袋换了两次肩，赤脚踩过石板时脚底发麻。
  _effects: food; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-24_

- **1921.06** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/eraser-cut-in-half` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  教师儿子把Lena的橡皮切成两半，说穷人的橡皮本来就只有半块。Lena没有还手，把半块橡皮收进铅笔盒。第二天Lena发现自己的作业本被撕了两页，Elisabeth Berg问是谁干的，全班都看着窗外。
  _effects: mood -0.08; flag +bullied_by_teachers_son; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1921.07** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  “Notwendigkeit”少了一个字母，Maria Moser 2没说话，只敲了敲桌角。Lena回家时手指上全是墨水，母亲问起，只说写了点作业。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33_

- **1921.09** `p9-childhood-school.truant-prater/truant-river-dark` tags: childhood, school, teacher, peers, lessons, truancy
  河面上最后一条船过去，灯一盏盏亮起来。Lena把两赫勒烤土豆的空纸袋揉成一团，塞进Elisabeth Bauer的口袋。到家时家里人正站在门口，手扶着门框。家里人闻了闻Lena的头发，又看了看鞋上的河泥，什么也没问，只把门开到最大。
  _effects: cash -0.02 (花两赫勒买了烤土豆); mood -0.05; relation acquaintance with person-elisabeth-bauer_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  Lena把错题纸举到讲台边，话说到一半就低下去了。Elisabeth Berg放下红笔，说“明早提前来”。第二天Lena摸黑走到Leopoldstädter Volksschule，教室门已经开了，炉子冒着青烟。Elisabeth Berg把竖式写在黑板上，先擦掉一半，让Lena补上，再一笔一笔讲为什么。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1922.11** `r1-child.punishment-peas::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/left-hand-for-the-ruler` tags: school, punishment, teacher, dialect, authority, university, culture, schooling
  Grete Stein举起戒尺时，Lena先把左手伸出去，说打右手明天写不了字。教室里有人笑，Grete Stein愣了一下，尺子落在左手心上，只打了两下。那天Lena用左手抄完了五十遍，字歪得像刚学走路的笔画。
  _effects: reputation 0.04; flag +offered_left_hand_to_ruler; skill; relation rival with person-grete-stein_

- **1923.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-dragged-docked-pay` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单太长，Lena拖着一角往绳子走，布边扫过煤渣，立刻黑了一道。Maria Kral弯腰看了看，脸沉下来，说这角得重洗，工钱扣一半。Lena接过一赫勒，蹲在盆边用清水搓那一道灰，搓到指节发红，水面上还浮着煤末。
  _effects: cash 0.01 (被扣后只剩一赫勒); mood -0.04999999999999999; relation acquaintance with person-maria-kral_

- **1923.09** `r14-edu.boarding-arrival::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/hazed-by-older-students` tags: education, boarding_isolation, boarding_school, childhood, university, culture, schooling, school
  Lena刚把箱子塞进床底，几个高年级学生就围过来，让Lena把家里带来的香肠交出来。Lena不给，箱子被踢翻，衬衫散了一地。Hermann Winter 2站在门边，想帮忙又退回去。熄灯后，Lena蹲在地上捡东西，膝盖磕在床脚上。
  _effects: mood -0.16; flag +hazed_at_boarding_school; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1924.01** `r13-edu.ask-teacher::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/note-under-inkwell` tags: education, teacher, help, school, confidence, university, culture, schooling
  Lena等到天黑，Maria Moser 2一直在改别的班的卷子，头也没抬。Lena把问题写在纸条上压在墨水瓶下，第二天发现纸条还在原处，只是背面多了一行字：“先看第三章。”Lena按纸条翻到第三章，发现那页夹着一张旧的辅助线图，图上的线画得很轻。
  _effects: skill_

- **1924.04** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, university, culture, schooling
  成绩单上差两分。Elisabeth Berg说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Friedrich Hofer来签字。Friedrich Hofer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Lena把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); mood -0.14999999999999997; condition; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill; relation guardian with person-friedrich-hofer_

- **1924.08** `p9-childhood-adolescence.courtyard-gang/gang-prater-locked-out` tags: adolescence, friendship, identity, work, school, secret, church
  Lena跟着Anton穿过普拉特大街，看人放完焰火才想起回家。院门已经上了闩，母亲的灯灭了，Lena在楼梯间坐到后半夜，膝盖冻得发僵。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Anton-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.020000000000000018_

- **1924.08** `r3-teen.disability-at-school::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/workshop-track` tags: disability, school, injury, access, institution, university, culture, schooling
  校长把Friedrich Hofer叫来，说学校没有坡道，不如早点去刷坊学门手艺。Friedrich Hofer看着Lena的腿，点了头。第二天，Lena的课桌被搬到走廊尽头，桌上放着一张刷坊的地址。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +pushed_to_workshop_track; role; education apprenticeship_

- **1924.08** `statistical_infection`
  Lena在Leopoldstädter Zinshaus, gemeinsamer Gang病倒了，肺炎让身体发热、虚弱，接下来的日子只能卧床。

- **1924.09** `statistical_illness_worsened`
  Lena的肺炎没有完全好转，咳嗽和发热又拖了一个月，家里人轮流守着。

- **1924.11** `statistical_recovery`
  Lena从肺炎中慢慢恢复，能下床喝水，但身体还虚弱。

- **1925.01** `r14-edu.boarding-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/letter-read-aloud` tags: education, boarding_isolation, letter, childhood, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Hermann Lechner查房时从枕头套里翻出那封信，在晨会上念了两段。念到“这里的饭像喂牲口”时，下面有人笑。Lena站在队列里，看见Hermann Winter 2低着头。散会后，Hermann Lechner让Lena把信抄十遍，贴在布告栏上。
  _effects: mood -0.18000000000000005; reputation -0.1; flag +letter_read_aloud; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:001890_
