- **1917.05** `p9-childhood-household.fever-night/hospital-deposit` tags: childhood, family, household, home, siblings, illness, neighbors
  围巾从Hedwig的头一直裹到脚，Josefine Hofer抱着人下楼，在电车踏板上差点滑倒。急诊窗口先收押金，Josefine Hofer把内袋里的票子一张张抹平，数出六克朗。Hedwig躺在观察室的窄床上，头顶的灯管嗡嗡响，Josefine Hofer的手一直按着被角。

  Josefine Hofer带Hedwig到Leopoldstädter Volksschule报名。Emil Hofer在名册上写下孩子的姓名和出生月份，收了两克朗书本费，让孩子第二天带一块石板来。
  _effects: education primary; cash -2 (school books（其中2克朗记入债务）); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8_

- **1918.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-borrowed-from-teacher` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  Grete Stein用拇指摸了摸石板上的裂缝，没说话，从讲台抽屉最底下抽出一块旧石板，木框还在，边角被磨得发亮。借的条件是每天放学擦黑板、倒炉灰。Hedwig把石板抱在怀里，当晚就用湿布擦了三遍，第二天袖口全是白粉。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; health -0.010000000000000009 (每天多留倒炉灰，沾上粉尘)_

- **1918.11** `vienna-early-childhood.fever-night/doctor-bill` tags: childhood, family, home, street
  Hedwig烧得嘴唇发干，Josefine Hofer把湿布敷上去，凉了再换，撑到窗外发白才去请医生。医生把听诊器贴在Hedwig背上，留下三包药粉，收了一古尔登。Josefine Hofer从围裙内袋里摸出硬币，一枚一枚排在桌上，差一点不够。
  _effects: cash -1 (医生的诊金（其中1克朗记入债务）); health 0.06 (服药后退烧); mood -0.04999999999999999_

- **1919.01** `vienna-life-school-day/punished` tags: childhood, school, hunger
  放学路上，Hedwig把手揣在口袋里，碰一下就疼。回到家门口，Hedwig先把手背在身后，等Josefine Hofer转身才敢看；那道红印到晚上还没有消。
  _effects: condition; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1919.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.stay-after-school/stay-early-morning` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, teacher, learning
  Hedwig把错题纸举到讲台边，话说到一半就低下去了。Elisabeth Berg放下红笔，说“明早提前来”。第二天Hedwig摸黑走到Leopoldstädter Volksschule，教室门已经开了，炉子冒着青烟。Elisabeth Berg把竖式写在黑板上，先擦掉一半，让Hedwig补上，再一笔一笔讲为什么。
  _effects: skill; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15_

- **1919.09** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.fail` tags: school, social, childhood
  Hedwig拼到一半停住，Grete Stein的尺子落在桌角上。放学后楼梯间只剩Hedwig一个人，把“Notwendigkeit”抄了二十遍，墨水瓶见了底。
  _effects: mood -0.06; skill; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein_

- **1920.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.school-slate/slate-homemade-wood-ash` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, supplies, education
  Josefine Hofer把空口袋翻给Hedwig看，里面只有线头和一枚扣子。Hedwig没再要，去木料堆挑了块没毛刺的薄板，用指头抹上湿煤灰，写一个字，抹一次。到Leopoldstädter Volksschule时木板边角已经把书包染黑，后排有人笑了一声，Hedwig把木板往桌肚里塞了塞。
  _effects: skill; mood -0.04; relation kin with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1920.05** `r1-child.epidemic-closure::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-school/temperature-line-in-soot` tags: school, epidemic, closure, labour, quarantine, medical, university, knowledge
  黑板旁贴了张告示：猩红热，停课两周。Emil Hofer让每个孩子带一张纸回家，上面抄着“每日体温”。Hedwig不会写“体温”，Josefine Hofer用灶灰在桌上画了一道线，说高过这条就说话。
  _effects: condition; flag +school_closed_for_scarlet_fever; skill; education primary_

- **1920.09** `vienna-school-childhood.soup-kitchen/soup-kitchen.spill` tags: school, social, childhood
  锅沿太烫，Hedwig手一松，汤泼在鞋面上。Elisabeth Berg拽着Hedwig到水龙头下冲脚，Lena蹲在地上刮面包屑。晚饭的汤没了，鞋还湿着。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; relation friend with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912_

- **1921.01** `vienna-life-child-labour/folds-notices` tags: childhood, labour, piecework
  Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne把一捆教区通告交给Josefine Hofer带回家。Hedwig放学后在厨房桌上折页到夜里，手指沾满油墨，两周后交回六千张，拿到三克朗二十赫勒；钱交给Josefine Hofer时，Hedwig的指尖已经洗不干净，指甲缝里留着一圈黑；家里从此多了一个能挣几个铜板的孩子。
  _effects: cash 3.2 (folding parish notices); condition; role_

- **1921.01** `r13-edu.school-roll-call::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/attends-through-frost` tags: education, school, attendance, childhood, poverty, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig踩着结冰的台阶进教室，第三个空位还空着。Elisabeth Berg让Hedwig把昨天的课文背完，又用戒尺敲了敲黑板，叫全班跟着念。放学时名字被写在红榜末尾，Hedwig把冻僵的手指揣进袖口，觉得明天还能来。放学路上，Hedwig把冻裂的手贴在热煤渣上。
  _effects: mood 0.03; flag +school_attendance_streak; skill_

- **1921.03** `vienna-childhood-friends.secret-kiosk/traded-for-picture` tags: childhood, social, school, street
  报亭后面的墙根下，Hedwig把咬了一半的糖递过去，换回一张火车头画片。糖纸被风吹到摊主的脚边。Josefine Hofer弯腰捡起来，把画片从Hedwig手里抽走，说先把地扫了。Hedwig扫到墙根，Josefine Hofer才把画片还回来。
  _effects: mood -0.03; relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1921.04** `r1-child.bully-teacher-son::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/eraser-cut-in-half` tags: bullying, school, teacher, class, fear, university, culture, schooling
  教师儿子把Hedwig的橡皮切成两半，说穷人的橡皮本来就只有半块。Hedwig没有还手，把半块橡皮收进铅笔盒。第二天Hedwig发现自己的作业本被撕了两页，Elisabeth Berg问是谁干的，全班都看着窗外。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +bullied_by_teachers_son; skill; relation rival with person-phase15-Klara-world-vienna-1912_

- **1922.01** `p9-gap-childhood-chores.laundry-help/laundry-three-heller` tags: childhood, household, chores, school, task, daily, laundry, work
  床单浸了碱水，沉得拽不动。Hedwig站在木凳上，把湿布一点点递过绳，水顺着肘弯滴进袖口。Josefine Hofer在绳头数夹子，最后一条挂好，从围裙口袋里摸出三枚赫勒，按进Hedwig掌心。晚上洗手时，虎口那道口子被水一蜇，Hedwig才看见。
  _effects: cash 0.03 (帮邻居晾床单挣的三赫勒); relation acquaintance with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1922.05** `vienna-school-childhood.spelling-bee/spelling-bee.win` tags: school, social, childhood
  拼写比赛结束时，Grete Stein敲了敲讲台说“记住这个词”。Hedwig袖口上多了一枚小十字章，回家路上Luise Berg追着要看，最后用三颗李子换了眼福。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; mood 0.1; relation acquaintance with person-grete-stein; food_

- **1922.09** `vienna-childhood-street.market-errand/errand-bullied` tags: childhood, street, social, home
  市场拱廊下晒不到太阳，Hedwig却被堵出一头汗。带子越挣越紧，肩膀在柱子上磕了一下。Mitzi从人群里挤过来，先看了看Hedwig的肩，又看了看柱子上的结，拿出小刀割断。两个大孩子骂了一句，Mitzi把Hedwig拉到菜篮后面。
  _effects: relation friend with person-phase15-Mitzi-world-vienna-1912; mood -0.05; condition_

- **1923.01** `r14-edu.music-lessons::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/rejected-for-small-hands` tags: education, arts_training, music, craft, university, culture, schooling, school
  Maria Moser 2看了Hedwig的手，说指头太短，够不到八度，学下去也是白费。Hedwig把手收进袖子里，回家后把琴谱还回去，放在琴房的窗台上。那天晚上，Hedwig在院子里坐了很久，听见隔壁有人在练同一支曲子。
  _effects: mood -0.10000000000000002; flag +rejected_by_music_teacher; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-41_

- **1924.01** `kernel-edu.apply-for-scholarship::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/edu-apply-scholarship-awarded` tags: task, adolescence, school, scholarship, papers, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig把成绩单、洗礼证明和Friedrich Hofer的收入证明摊在桌上，Maria Moser 2逐张检查，缺一张就划掉重来。Emil Hofer在窗口盖了三个章，收下两克朗印花税。六周后，通知来了：每年二百四十克朗，先发三十。
  _effects: education secondary; cash 28 (first scholarship payment after the stamp fee); role; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-8; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-33; flag +scholarship_awarded_

- **1924.11** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-school/mother-fined` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, university, culture, schooling
  Hermann Lechner判Josefine Hofer监管不力，罚了六克朗，从下个月的救济金里扣。Josefine Hofer在法庭上数了三遍口袋，最后把戒指摘下来放在桌上。Hermann Lechner看了一眼，说“这个不收”，让书记员记下分期付款。Hedwig一直低着头，看见Josefine Hofer的手在抖。
  _effects: cash -6 (长椅上晃着两条够不着地的腿（其中5.3克朗记入债务）); condition; flag +parent_fined_for_child; relation rival with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the fine will be deducted from the relief payment”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-37; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-40_

- **1925.01** `r3-teen.dormitory-theft::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/blamed` tags: education, dormitory, theft, trust, institution, reform, medical, schooling
  Elisabeth Berg在Hedwig的洗衣袋里找到了Hermann Weiss丢的表，表链上还缠着一根红线。全宿舍站成一排，Hermann Weiss没有看Hedwig。她说不是自己拿的，声音在瓷砖墙上撞回来，显得很轻。
  _effects: reputation -0.1; condition; flag +accused_of_dormitory_theft; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46_

- **1925.01** `r14-edu.entrance-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/short-by-two-points` tags: education, exam_failure, entrance_exam, shame, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Hedwig在放榜墙前从头看到尾，没有找到自己的名字。差两分。Elisabeth Berg把Hedwig叫到办公室，说作文写得太满，结尾却空了。Hermann Winter 2的名字在第二行，用红笔圈着。Hedwig回家后把课本码齐，最上面一本压着没写完的志愿表。补考费一克朗，Hedwig没有去交。
  _effects: cash -1 (放榜墙上的空白); mood -0.14; flag +failed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1925.01** `kernel-edu.apply-for-scholarship::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/edu-apply-scholarship-incomplete` tags: task, adolescence, school, scholarship, papers, university, culture, schooling
  Gustav Berg把材料推回来，说洗礼证明上的名字和户口册对不上，要先去找教堂更正。Hedwig跑了三趟，教堂的副本要两克朗。等证明补齐，名额已经发完。Hedwig学会在每张证明背后写下日期。
  _effects: cash -4 (copies and stamps for the corrected certificate（其中2.6克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.03; flag +scholarship_application_incomplete; relation acquaintance with person-gustav-berg; skill_

- **1925.05** `r3-teen.first-love::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/reciprocated` tags: love, courtship, school, tram, secrecy, reform, medical, schooling
  Anton Winter 2把回信写在电车月票背面：星期三，六点，第二站。Hedwig提前半小时到，站在报亭旁边，把那张月票看了十几遍。Anton Winter 2从电车上下来时，围巾被风吹到脸上，两个人都笑了。
  _effects: mood 0.12; flag +first_love_reciprocated; relation partner with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-45_

  Grete Hofer看了看Hedwig的手，说太小，拿不稳凿子。Hedwig回家没有提这件事，第二天去码头找零工。
  _effects: mood -0.08000000000000002; flag +apprenticeship_refused; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-9_

- **1926.01** `r14-edu.retake-examination::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/failed-again` tags: education, exam_failure, retake, persistence, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Hedwig又差一分。Elisabeth Berg把卷子合上，说不是不会，是每到大题就手抖。回家的路上，Hedwig在桥边站了很久，把准考证撕成两半，又蹲下去一片片捡起来。第二天，Hedwig去印刷所问还招不招学徒。这次补考又交了两克朗。
  _effects: cash -2 (第二次走进同一间考场（其中2克朗记入债务）); mood -0.16000000000000003; flag +failed_retake_exam_

  _effects: employment Buchbinderhilfe @ org-winter-print; condition_

  检查员来的时候，Otto Winter让Hedwig和Anton免费坐一趟，说帮忙试试新座椅。座舱升到顶，检查员在下面喊“晃一晃”。Hedwig抓着扶手摇了两下，听见铁架发出一种空心的响声。检查员在本子上写了一个字，Otto Winter的脸就沉了。
  _effects: reputation 0.03; condition; flag +found_loose_ferris_bolt; relation acquaintance with person-otto-winter; knowledge “one of the ferris wheel bolts is loose”_

  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003066_

- **1928.01** `vienna-life-young-worker/joins-union` tags: adolescence, labour, union
  工会的人在厂门口拦住Hedwig，说学徒工也该有工票和保险。Hedwig每月交一克朗会费，把会员证塞进上衣内袋。
  _effects: role; cash -1 (union dues); relation acquaintance with person-alois-lechner_

- **1928.01** `state_tick:night-school/annual/held` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig又撑过一年夜校；工位和课桌仍然互相偷时间，但进度在缓慢累积。
  _effects: state_parameter night-school.progress 0 -> 15_

- **1928.01** `r14-edu.scholarship-examination::ctx-vienna-university-1912::milieu-university/lost` tags: education, scholarship_trial, exam, poverty, ambition, university, culture, schooling
  Hedwig差两分落榜。Maria Moser 2把卷子摊在讲台上，说作文立意不坏，只是字太挤。回家的路上，Hedwig把准考证折成四折，塞进鞋垫底下；晚饭时谁也没提考场，只听见勺子碰碗沿。第二天，Hedwig照常去上课，座位往后挪了一排。
  _effects: flag +scholarship_lost; skill_

- **1928.03** `r1-cul.fan-letter::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/hid-letter-in-bookshop` tags: culture, letters, fandom, literacy, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig没有寄信，把信塞进Otto Winter常去的书店，夹在一本旧书里。三个月后，书被卖掉，信也不见了；Berta Hofer说那家书店上个月清过仓，旧书论斤卖。Hedwig去废纸站找了一下午，只找到半张写着我不敢寄的纸。
  _effects: cash -1 (寄给作者的信); mood -0.019999999999999907; condition; flag +hid_letter_in_bookshop; knowledge “the bookshop sold its old stock by weight”_

- **1929.01** `state_expire:night-school` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick, state:night-school
  Hedwig的「夜校生」到期；相关安排结束。
  _effects: state_remove night-school state:world-vienna-1912:81_

- **1929.01** `r13-edu.exam-eve::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/passed-entrance-exam` tags: education, exam, study, poverty, adolescence, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig把煤油灯芯剪短，省下油熬到后半夜。代数题做错三遍，Hedwig就用冷水拍脸，再把公式抄到手腕内侧。考试那天，监考把袖口翻了一遍，Hedwig还是把最后一道题解完了。放榜那天，Hedwig在人群后面站了很久，才敢往前挤。
  _effects: mood 0.06000000000000005; flag +passed_entrance_exam; skill_

- **1929.02** `recipe-education.scholarship-exam::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/passed` tags: education, exam, class, poverty, ambition, reform, medical, schooling
  Hedwig用Luise Winter借的钢笔写完最后一题，笔尖在纸上划出一道细缝。放榜那天，Elisabeth Berg把Hedwig的名字念了两遍，Luise Winter站在人群后面，先鼓掌，再低头看自己的鞋。
  _effects: reputation 0.08; relation colleague with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-15; education secondary_

- **1929.07** `r15-education.entry-offer::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-university/full-scholarship` tags: education, scholarship, opportunity, medical, university, knowledge, student, research
  Hedwig的成绩打动了本地学校，拿到一份覆盖学费的奖学金；家里第一次不用为这笔钱吵到半夜。
  _effects: state_add scholarship-holder state:world-vienna-1912:97_

- **1929.08** `r3-crime.juvenile-court::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::court-without-bars/mother-fined` tags: crime, youth, court, family, school, reform, medical, schooling
  Friedrich Hofer判Josefine Hofer监管不力，罚了六克朗，从下个月的救济金里扣。Josefine Hofer在法庭上数了三遍口袋，最后把戒指摘下来放在桌上。Friedrich Hofer看了一眼，说“这个不收”，让书记员记下分期付款。Hedwig一直低着头，看见Josefine Hofer的手在抖。
  _effects: cash -6 (长椅上晃着两条够不着地的腿（其中6克朗记入债务）); relation rival with person-josefine-hofer; knowledge “the fine will be deducted from the relief payment”; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-56_

- **1929.12** `r13-turn.exam-failure::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school::reform-open-secondary/retake-fee` tags: education, exam, failure, school, work, reform, medical, schooling
  成绩单上差两分。Josef Weiss 2说可以补考，但要先交一克朗卷子费，还得让Josefine Hofer来签字。Josefine Hofer在办公室门口把帽子捏变了形，说家里拿不出这份钱。Hedwig把错题抄到烟盒背面，第二天照样去上课，只是不再举手。
  _effects: cash -1 (落榜名单上的第三个名字（其中1克朗记入债务）); condition; skill; relation guardian with person-josefine-hofer_

- **1930.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-medical-1930::milieu-university/sold-the-letter` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, medical, university
  Anna Moser 2把Hedwig托付保管的一封旧信卖给了收旧纸的人。信里提到几个已经不用的名字，买主又把信转给了另一个买主。Hedwig再见到那封信时，它被压在一块玻璃下面，标价是Hedwig半个月的工钱。
  _effects: cash -1 (买回自己的旧信（其中1克朗记入债务）); reputation -0.05; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28; knowledge “我写的一封旧信被人压在玻璃下面标价出售。”; flag +private_letter_sold; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-28_

- **1930.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Anna Moser 2第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 0 -> 1; mood -0.06000000000000005_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004125_

- **1930.01** `state_tick:scholarship-holder/annual-renewal/revoked` tags: scholarship-holder, education, scholarship, means_tested, state, state_tick, night-school
  Hedwig的奖学金被取消，学校把过去一年的费用开成一张账单。
  _effects: state_transition scholarship-holder -> night-school_

- **1930.01** `kernel-social.lending::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/lending.refused` tags: task, task:social, social, lending, debt, neighbor, money, everyday
  Hedwig说自己也等钱交房租，把口袋翻给Lena看。Lena没有再求，转身去敲另一扇门；第二天，楼道里有人说Hedwig见死不救，Hedwig听见了，只把煤筐往门里挪了挪。那天夜里，两家都早早关了灯。
  _effects: relation acquaintance with person-phase15-Lena-world-vienna-1912; role; reputation -0.03; flag +loan_refused; knowledge “邻居转身去敲了别人的门，楼道里开始议论这件事。”_

- **1930.03** `vienna-parish-aid.sick-call/sick-call.help-in` tags: faith, parish, mutual_aid, poverty, family
  Hedwig和Anna Bauer 2一起上楼，门没锁，屋里一股酸味。病人裹着毯子坐在床沿咳嗽，炉子只剩灰。Hedwig去楼下讨了几块煤核，蹲着把火生起来，又用带来的面粉搅了一小锅稀粥。药粉冲开，碗底沉着白渣，Hedwig先垫了药钱，说以后再说。
  _effects: food; cash -0.6 (替病人垫上药粉钱（其中0.6克朗记入债务）); relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3; flag +parish_sick_visitor; relation acquaintance with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-3_

- **1931.01** `curse15-betrayal.name-on-the-list::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/the-accusation-fails` tags: betrayal, politics, investigation, curse, denunciation, secret, reform, medical
  Hermann Weiss在众人面前说Hedwig用诅咒害人。没有人接话，因为上个月Hermann Weiss也用同样的话说过另一个人。事情没有闹大，但Hedwig发现，从此每次走进那间屋子，都有人先看Hedwig的手。
  _effects: reputation -0.03; relation rival with person-materialized-world-vienna-1912-46; state_add suspected-witchcraft state:world-vienna-1912:125; flag +accusation_echoed_

- **1931.01** `state_reaction:cursed-by-wish/curse-betrayal` tags: cursed-by-wish, wish, curse, supernatural, revenge, special, state, state_reaction
  背叛发生后的夜里，Hedwig自己发起了高烧；Hermann Weiss第二天照常出门。没有人把这两件事放在一起。
  _effects: state_parameter cursed-by-wish.triggers 1 -> 2_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:004509_

- **1931.01** `state_tick:night-school/annual/held` tags: night-school, education, night_school, work, state, state_tick
  Hedwig又撑过一年夜校；工位和课桌仍然互相偷时间，但进度在缓慢累积。
  _effects: state_parameter night-school.progress 0 -> 15_

- **1931.01** `r13-fam.repair-friendship::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-tenement/argument-reopened` tags: task, social, friendship, repair, trust, reform, medical, schooling
  话说到一半，Mitzi Lechner又把三年前的旧账翻出来，声音越来越高。Hedwig把杯子放下，没有还嘴，只把桌上的水渍擦干。Mitzi Lechner忽然停下来，问Hedwig是不是觉得自己有理；Hedwig说不是，只是不想再摔第二个杯子。
  _effects: condition; flag +friendship_truce; relation friend with person-mitzi-lechner_

- **1932.01** `r14-edu.failed-scholar-teaches::ctx-vienna-reform-1930::milieu-school/private-tutor` tags: education, exam_failure, teaching, teacher_recommendation, reform, medical, schooling, school
  Franz Huber翻完Hedwig的成绩单，说没有师范文凭，不能上讲台。Hedwig在校门口贴了一张家教纸条，第二天就有人来问。第一家是个开杂货铺的寡妇，孩子算术差，Hedwig每晚去教一个钟头，换一顿晚饭和两克朗，一个月攒下四克朗。
  _effects: cash 4 (落榜的人站上讲台); flag +tutored_for_a_living; role_
  _causes: evt:world-vienna-1912:003239_

- **1932.10** `vienna-life-work-injury/fatal-accident` tags: adulthood, labour, injury, death
  压板落下时Hedwig没能把手抽出来。工友把人抬到门口，等救护车到时已经没有了呼吸；Buchdruckerei Winter & Söhne当天下午派人送来三十克朗抚恤金，车间里的机器停了一个钟头，又响起来，像什么都没有发生过。
  _effects: death (crushing injury at the printing press); cash 30 (employer death benefit)_
